```
   158→# models = ["llama-3.3-70b-versatile"]
   159→# alias = "groq"
   160→
   161→# ── DeepSeek ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
   162→# [providers.deepseek]
   163→# enabled = true
   164→# api_key = "..."                             # Or set DEEPSEEK_API_KEY env var
   165→# models = ["deepseek-chat"]
   166→# base_url = "https://api.deepseek.com"
   167→# alias = "deepseek"
   168→
   169→# ── Fireworks ────────────────────────────────────────────────
   170→# [providers.fireworks]
   171→# enabled = true
   172→# api_key = "..."                             # Or set FIREWORKS_API_KEY env var
   173→# models = ["accounts/fireworks/routers/kimi-k2p5-turbo"]
   174→# fetch_models = true                          # Set false to skip remote discovery
   175→# base_url = "https://api.fireworks.ai/inference/v1"
   176→# alias = "fireworks"
   177→
   178→# ── xAI (Grok) ────────────────────────────────────────────────
   179→# [providers.xai]
   180→# enabled = true
   181→# api_key = "..."                             # Or set XAI_API_KEY env var
   182→# models = ["grok-3-mini"]
   183→# alias = "xai"
   184→
   185→# ── OpenRouter (multi-provider gateway) ───────────────────────
   186→# [providers.openrouter]
   187→# enabled = true
   188→# api_key = "..."                             # Or set OPENROUTER_API_KEY env var
   189→# models = ["anthropic/claude-3.5-sonnet"]    # Any model IDs on OpenRouter
   190→# base_url = "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1"
   191→
   192→# ── Moonshot (Kimi) ─────────────────────────────────────────
   193→[providers.moonshot]
   194→# enabled = true
   195→# api_key = "..."                             # Or set MOONSHOT_API_KEY env var
   196→models = ["kimi-k2.5"]                        # Preferred models shown first
   197→# base_url = "https://api.moonshot.ai/v1"
   198→# alias = "moonshot"
   199→
   200→[providers.ollama]
   201→# base_url = "http://localhost:11434"
   202→# models = ["llama3.2", "qwen2.5:7b"]         # Optional preferred models; installed models are discovered dynamically
   203→
   204→[providers.local-llm]
   205→# models = ["qwen2.5-coder-7b-q4_k_m"]        # Optional; configure local models in onboarding
   206→
   207→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   208→# MODEL OVERRIDES (GLOBAL)
   209→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   210→# Override context window sizes for specific models across all providers.
   211→# Provider-scoped overrides ([providers.<name>.model_overrides.<id>]) take precedence.
   212→#
   213→# [models.claude-opus-4-6]
   214→# context_window = 1_000_000                  # Override the built-in heuristic
   215→#
   216→# [models.glm-5-turbo]
   217→# context_window = 200_000
   218→
   219→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   220→# CHAT SETTINGS
   221→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   222→
   223→[chat]
   224→message_queue_mode = "followup"   # Default: process queued messages one-by-one after the current run.
   225→                                  # How to handle messages during an active agent run:
   226→                                  #   "followup" - Queue messages, replay one-by-one after run
   227→                                  #   "collect"  - Buffer messages, concatenate as single message
   228→# prompt_memory_mode = "live-reload"  # How MEMORY.md reaches the prompt:
   229→                                      #   "live-reload"            - Re-read MEMORY.md before each turn
   230→                                      #   "frozen-at-session-start" - Freeze the first MEMORY.md snapshot per session
   231→# workspace_file_max_chars = 32000  # Optional: per-file prompt cap for AGENTS.md / TOOLS.md before truncation.
   232→# priority_models = ["claude-opus-4-5", "gpt-5.2", "gemini-3-flash"]  # Optional: models to pin first in selectors
   233→# allowed_models = ["gpt 5.2"]  # Legacy field (currently ignored).
   234→
   235→# ── Compaction ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   236→# Strategy used to shrink a session when its context window fills up, or when
   237→# a user invokes `/compact`. Four modes are available — pick the one that
   238→# matches your cost/fidelity trade-off. See docs/src/compaction.md for a full
   239→# comparison table and picking guide.
   240→#
   241→# Modes:
   242→#   "deterministic"        (default) Zero LLM calls. Replaces the entire
   243→#                          history with a single extracted summary message
   244→#                          (counts, tool names, file paths, recent user
   245→#                          requests, head+tail timeline). Fast, free, offline,
   246→#                          low fidelity. Best for short chat channels and
   247→#                          cost-sensitive deployments.
   248→#
   249→#   "recency_preserving"   Zero LLM calls. Keeps head (system prompt +
   250→#                          first exchange) and recent tail verbatim (sized
   251→#                          by token budget). Collapses the middle into a
   252→#                          short marker message and replaces any bulky
   253→#                          tool-result content in the retained slice with a
   254→#                          placeholder. Repairs orphaned tool_use /
   255→#                          tool_result pairs so strict providers accept the
   256→#                          retry. Mid fidelity, free.
   257→#
   258→#   "structured"           Head + LLM structured summary + tail. Same
   259→#                          boundary logic as recency_preserving, but the
   260→#                          middle is summarised with a single LLM call
   261→#                          using a Goal / Progress / Decisions / Files /
   262→#                          Next Steps template (same convention as
   263→#                          hermes-agent and openclaw safeguard). Iterative
   264→#                          re-compaction preserves prior summary sections.
   265→#                          Highest fidelity, costs a summary LLM call per
   266→#                          compaction. Falls back to recency_preserving on
   267→#                          LLM failure or empty summary.
   268→#
   269→#   "llm_replace"          Replaces the entire history with a single
   270→#                          LLM-generated summary. Pre-PR-#653 behaviour.
   271→#                          Best for maximum token reduction when the session
   272→#                          provider is cheap and the tail isn't worth
   273→#                          preserving.
   274→#
   275→[chat.compaction]
   276→mode = "deterministic"              # "deterministic" | "recency_preserving" | "structured" | "llm_replace"
   277→# threshold_percent = 0.95          # Fires auto-compaction when the next request is estimated to exceed
   278→                                    # this fraction of the model context window. Also multiplied into
   279→                                    # the verbatim tail budget for recency_preserving / structured modes.
   280→                                    # Default matches the pre-PR-#653 hardcoded trigger so upgrades are
   281→                                    # behaviour-neutral. Range: 0.10–0.95. Lower = more aggressive compaction.
   282→# protect_head = 3                  # Number of leading messages kept verbatim by recency/structured modes.
   283→# protect_tail_min = 20             # Floor for tail messages kept verbatim (recency/structured modes).
   284→# tail_budget_ratio = 0.20          # Size of the verbatim tail as a fraction of threshold_percent × context_window.
   285→                                    # Example (defaults): 200K context × 0.95 × 0.20 = 38K tokens of tail preserved.
   286→# tool_prune_char_threshold = 200   # Tool-result content longer than this is replaced with a placeholder
   287→                                    # when recency/structured modes prune the middle region.
   288→# summary_model = "openrouter/google/gemini-2.5-flash"  # RESERVED — auxiliary-model subsystem not yet wired
   289→                                                         # (tracked by beads issue moltis-8me). Setting this
   290→                                                         # today emits a one-shot runtime WARN and has no
   291→                                                         # effect on the structured / llm_replace strategies,
   292→                                                         # which still use the session's primary provider.
   293→# max_summary_tokens = 4096         # RESERVED — see summary_model above. Default is 4096 but the value
   294→                                    # is not yet applied to the streaming summary call.
   295→# show_settings_hint = true         # Append "Change chat.compaction.mode in moltis.toml…" to every
   296→                                    # compaction notice (web UI card + channel messages). Default: true.
   297→                                    # Set to false once you know the setting exists to hide the repetitive
   298→                                    # footer without losing the mode + token metadata.
   299→
   300→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   301→# SUB-AGENT SPAWN PRESETS (OPTIONAL)
   302→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   303→# Configure reusable presets for sub-agents spawned via the `spawn_agent` tool.
   304→#
   305→# ⚠️  SCOPE: `[agents.presets.*]` applies ONLY to sub-agents spawned via the
   306→# `spawn_agent` tool. The `tools.allow` / `tools.deny` fields under a preset
   307→# do NOT filter tools for the main agent session. To allow/deny tools for the
   308→# main session, use the `[tools.policy]` section further down this file.
   309→# `[agents] default_preset` likewise only selects the sub-agent preset used
   310→# when `spawn_agent.preset` is omitted — it does not apply to the main session.
   311→#
   312→# [agents]
   313→# default_preset = "research"      # Sub-agent preset used when spawn_agent.preset is omitted
   314→#
   315→# [agents.presets.research]
   316→# model = "openai/gpt-5.2"
   317→# allow_tools = ["web_search", "web_fetch", "sessions_send", "task_list"]
   318→# deny_tools = ["exec"]
   319→# delegate_only = false
   320→# system_prompt_suffix = "Focus on gathering and summarizing evidence."
   321→
   322→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   323→# TOOLS
   324→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   325→
   326→[tools]
   327→agent_timeout_secs = 600          # Max seconds for an agent run (0 = no timeout)
   328→agent_max_iterations = 25         # Max LLM/tool loop iterations before stopping
   329→agent_max_auto_continues = 2      # Auto-continue nudges when model stops mid-task (0 = off)
   330→agent_auto_continue_min_tool_calls = 3  # Min tool calls before auto-continue can trigger
   331→max_tool_result_bytes = 50000     # Max bytes per tool result before truncation (50KB)
   332→# registry_mode = "full"          # "full" = all schemas every turn, "lazy" = tool_search discovery
   333→agent_loop_detector_window = 3    # Fire intervention after N identical failing tool calls in a row (0 = disable)
   334→agent_loop_detector_strip_tools_on_second_fire = true  # On second consecutive fire, strip tool schemas for one turn to force a text response
   335→tool_result_compaction_ratio = 75     # % of context_window before oldest tool results are compacted (0 = disable)
   336→preemptive_overflow_ratio = 90        # % of context_window before hard ContextWindowExceeded error
   337→compaction_min_iterations = 3         # Min loop iterations before per-iteration compaction can fire
   338→
   339→# ── Maps ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   340→
   341→[tools.maps]
   342→provider = "google_maps"          # Map provider used by show_map:
   343→                                  #   "google_maps" (default)
   344→                                  #   "apple_maps"
   345→                                  #   "openstreetmap"
   346→
   347→# ── Native filesystem tools (Read/Write/Edit/MultiEdit/Glob/Grep) ─────────────
   348→# All fields are optional. Defaults are conservative — the fs tools work
   349→# out of the box with no configuration.
   350→
   351→[tools.fs]
   352→# workspace_root = "/home/user/projects/my-app"
   353→                                  # Absolute path used as the default search root
   354→                                  # for Glob/Grep when the LLM call omits `path`.
   355→                                  # Unset → calls without an explicit `path` are
   356→                                  # rejected with a clear error.
   357→allow_paths = []                  # Absolute path globs the fs tools are allowed
   358→                                  # to access. Empty = no allowlist (all paths OK).
   359→                                  # Evaluated after canonicalization so symlinks
   360→                                  # can't escape the list.
   361→                                  # Example: ["/data/home/*", "/srv/workspace/**"]
   362→deny_paths = []                   # Absolute path globs the fs tools must refuse.
   363→                                  # Deny wins over allow. Also evaluated after
   364→                                  # canonicalization. Example:
   365→                                  #   ["/data/secrets/**", "**/.env*", "/etc/**"]
   366→track_reads = false               # Record per-session Read history for loop
   367→                                  # detection and the must_read_before_write
   368→                                  # invariant. Cheap in-memory map keyed by
   369→                                  # session_key.
   370→must_read_before_write = false    # Refuse Write/Edit/MultiEdit calls targeting
   371→                                  # files the session has not previously Read.
   372→                                  # Requires track_reads = true. Returns a typed
   373→                                  # must_read_before_write payload the LLM can
   374→                                  # branch on.
   375→require_approval = true           # When true, Write/Edit/MultiEdit pause for
   376→                                  # explicit operator approval before mutating.
   377→                                  # Uses the existing approval queue and will
   378→                                  # time out if nobody approves the request.
   379→max_read_bytes = 10485760         # Maximum bytes a single Read can return before
   380→                                  # the file is rejected with a typed "too_large"
   381→                                  # payload (10 MB).
   382→binary_policy = "reject"          # How to handle binary files in Read:
   383→                                  #   "reject" — return typed binary marker (default)
   384→                                  #   "base64" — return base64-encoded bytes in the
   385→                                  #              payload (still capped by max_read_bytes)
   386→respect_gitignore = true          # When true (default), Glob and Grep skip files
   387→                                  # ignored by .gitignore / .ignore / .git/info/exclude
   388→                                  # while walking. Set false to include ignored files.
   389→checkpoint_before_mutation = false  # When true, Write/Edit/MultiEdit call the
   390→                                  # existing CheckpointManager to snapshot the target
   391→                                  # file before mutating, so the LLM (or operator) can
   392→                                  # restore it later via `checkpoint_restore`. Default
   393→                                  # off because checkpoints grow with activity and
   394→                                  # size scales with file size.
   395→# context_window_tokens = 200000    # Model context window in tokens. When set, Read's
   396→                                  # per-call byte cap scales adaptively so a single
   397→                                  # Read uses at most ~20% of the model's working set
   398→                                  # (clamped to [50 KB, 512 KB]). Unset → fixed 256 KB.
   399→                                  # Typical values: 200000 for Claude Sonnet, 1000000
   400→                                  # for Claude Opus 4.6 1M, 128000 for GPT-4 Turbo.
   401→
   402→# ── Command Execution ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   403→
   404→[tools.exec]
   405→default_timeout_secs = 30         # Default timeout for commands
   406→max_output_bytes = 204800         # Max command output bytes (200KB)
   407→approval_mode = "on-miss"         # When to require approval:
   408→                                  #   "always"  - Always ask before running
   409→                                  #   "on-miss" - Ask if not in allowlist
   410→                                  #   "never"   - Never ask (for headless deployments)
   411→security_level = "allowlist"      # Security mode:
   412→                                  #   "permissive" - Allow most commands
   413→                                  #   "allowlist"  - Only allow listed commands
   414→                                  #   "strict"     - Very restrictive
   415→allowlist = []                    # Command patterns to allow (when security_level = "allowlist").
   416→                                  # With approval_mode = "never", a non-empty allowlist is enforced:
   417→                                  # commands that don't match are denied (safe bins still allowed).
   418→                                  # An empty allowlist in "never" mode is unrestricted.
   419→                                  # Example: ["git *", "npm *", "cargo *"]
   420→host = "local"                    # Where to run commands:
   421→                                  #   "local" - Run on this machine (default)
   422→                                  #   "node"  - Run on a connected Moltis node
   423→                                  #   "ssh"   - Run through the system ssh client
   424→# node = "mac-mini"               # Default node id/display name when host = "node"
   425→# ssh_target = "deploy@box"       # SSH host alias or user@host when host = "ssh"
   426→
   427→# ── Sandbox Configuration ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   428→# Commands run inside isolated containers for security.
   429→
   430→[tools.exec.sandbox]
   431→mode = "all"                      # Which commands to sandbox:
   432→                                  #   "off"      - No sandboxing (commands run on host)
   433→                                  #   "non-main" - Sandbox all except main session
   434→                                  #   "all"      - Sandbox everything (recommended)
   435→scope = "session"                 # Container lifecycle:
   436→                                  #   "command" - New container per command
   437→                                  #   "session" - Container per session (recommended)
   438→                                  #   "global"  - Single shared container
   439→workspace_mount = "ro"            # How to mount workspace in sandbox:
   440→                                  #   "ro"   - Read-only (safe; sandbox commands can read mounted files but cannot modify them)
   441→                                  #   "rw"   - Read-write (can modify files)
   442→                                  #   "none" - No mount
   443→# host_data_dir = "/host/path/data"  # Optional override if auto-detection cannot resolve the host-visible data dir
   444→home_persistence = "shared"       # Persist /home/sandbox across container recreation:
   445→                                  #   "off"     - Ephemeral home
   446→                                  #   "session" - Per-session persisted home
   447→                                  #   "shared"  - One shared persisted home (default)
   448→# shared_home_dir = "/path/to/shared-home"  # Host dir for shared persistence (default: data_dir()/sandbox/home/shared)
   449→backend = "auto"                  # Container backend:
   450→                                  #   "auto"            - Auto-detect (prefers Apple Container on macOS)
   451→                                  #   "docker"          - Use Docker
   452→                                  #   "apple-container" - Use Apple Container (macOS only)
   453→no_network = true                 # Disable network access in sandbox (recommended)
   454→# image = "custom-image:tag"      # Custom Docker image (default: auto-built)
   455→# container_prefix = "moltis"     # Prefix for container names
   456→
   457→# Packages installed in sandbox containers via apt-get.
   458→# This list is used to build the sandbox image. Customize as needed.
   459→packages = [
   460→    # Networking & HTTP
   461→    "curl",
   462→    "wget",
   463→    "ca-certificates",
   464→    "dnsutils",
   465→    "netcat-openbsd",
   466→    "openssh-client",
   467→    "iproute2",
   468→    "net-tools",
   469→    # Language runtimes
   470→    "python3",
   471→    "python3-dev",
   472→    "python3-pip",
   473→    "python3-venv",
   474→    "python-is-python3",
   475→    "nodejs",
   476→    "npm",
   477→    "ruby",
   478→    "ruby-dev",
   479→    "golang-go",
   480→    # Build toolchain & native deps
   481→    "build-essential",
   482→    "clang",
   483→    "libclang-dev",
   484→    "llvm-dev",
   485→    "pkg-config",
   486→    "libssl-dev",
   487→    "libsqlite3-dev",
   488→    "libyaml-dev",
   489→    "liblzma-dev",
   490→    "autoconf",
   491→    "automake",
   492→    "libtool",
   493→    "bison",
   494→    "flex",
   495→    "dpkg-dev",
   496→    "fakeroot",
   497→    # Compression & archiving
   498→    "zip",
   499→    "unzip",
   500→    "bzip2",
   501→    "xz-utils",
   502→    "p7zip-full",
   503→    "tar",
   504→    "zstd",
   505→    "lz4",
   506→    "pigz",
   507→    # Common CLI utilities
   508→    "git",
   509→    "gnupg2",
   510→    "jq",
   511→    "rsync",
   512→    "file",
   513→    "tree",
   514→    "sqlite3",
   515→    "sudo",
   516→    "locales",
   517→    "tzdata",
   518→    "shellcheck",
   519→    "patchelf",
   520→    "tmux",
   521→    # Text processing & search
   522→    "ripgrep",
   523→    # Browser automation dependencies
   524→    "chromium",
   525→    "libxss1",
   526→    "libnss3",
   527→    "libnspr4",
   528→    "libasound2t64",
   529→    "libatk1.0-0t64",
   530→    "libatk-bridge2.0-0t64",
   531→    "libcups2t64",
   532→    "libdrm2",
   533→    "libgbm1",
   534→    "libgtk-3-0t64",
   535→    "libxcomposite1",
   536→    "libxdamage1",
   537→    "libxfixes3",
   538→    "libxrandr2",
   539→    "libxkbcommon0",
   540→    "fonts-liberation",
   541→]
   542→
   543→# Resource limits for sandboxed execution (optional)
   544→[tools.exec.sandbox.resource_limits]
   545→# memory_limit = "512M"           # Memory limit (e.g., "512M", "1G", "2G")
   546→# cpu_quota = 0.5                 # CPU quota as fraction (0.5 = half a core, 2.0 = two cores)
   547→# pids_max = 100                  # Maximum number of processes
   548→
   549→# Tool policy overrides applied when commands run inside the sandbox (layer 6).
   550→# These narrow the effective policy for sandboxed execution only.
   551→# [tools.exec.sandbox.tools_policy]
   552→# allow = ["exec"]                # Only allow exec tool inside sandbox
   553→# deny = ["browser"]              # Deny browser tool inside sandbox
   554→
   555→# ── Tool Policy ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   556→# Control which tools the agent can use. Policies are layered (later wins for
   557→# allow, deny always accumulates across layers):
   558→#
   559→#   1. Global        — this section ([tools.policy])
   560→#   2. Per-provider  — [providers.<name>.policy]
   561→#   3. Per-agent     — [agents.presets.<id>.tools]
   562→#   4. Per-channel   — [channels.<type>.<account>.tools.groups.<chat_type>]
   563→#   5. Per-sender    — [...groups.<chat_type>.by_sender.<sender_id>]
   564→#   6. Sandbox       — [tools.exec.sandbox.tools_policy] (only when sandboxed)
   565→#
   566→# This is the base policy for all sessions. Provider and channel layers
   567→# narrow it further based on runtime context.
   568→
   569→[tools.policy]
   570→allow = []                        # Tools to always allow (e.g., ["exec", "web_fetch"])
   571→deny = []                         # Tools to always deny (e.g., ["browser"])
   572→# profile = "default"             # Named policy profile
   573→
   574→# ── Web Search ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   575→
   576→[tools.web.search]
   577→enabled = true                    # Enable web search tool
   578→provider = "brave"                # Search provider: "brave" or "perplexity"
   579→max_results = 5                   # Number of results to return (1-10)
   580→timeout_seconds = 30              # HTTP request timeout
   581→cache_ttl_minutes = 15            # Cache results for this many minutes (0 = no cache)
   582→duckduckgo_fallback = false       # Off by default; enable only if you want DDG fallback without API keys
   583→# api_key = "..."                 # Brave API key (or set BRAVE_API_KEY env var)
   584→
   585→# Perplexity-specific settings (when provider = "perplexity")
   586→[tools.web.search.perplexity]
   587→# api_key = "..."                 # Or set PERPLEXITY_API_KEY env var
   588→# base_url = "..."                # API base URL (auto-detected from key prefix)
   589→# model = "sonar"                 # Perplexity model to use
   590→
   591→# ── Web Fetch ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   592→
   593→[tools.web.fetch]
   594→enabled = true                    # Enable web fetch tool
   595→max_chars = 50000                 # Max characters to return from fetched content
   596→timeout_seconds = 30              # HTTP request timeout
   597→cache_ttl_minutes = 15            # Cache fetched pages for this many minutes (0 = no cache)
   598→max_redirects = 3                 # Maximum HTTP redirects to follow
   599→readability = true                # Use readability extraction for HTML (cleaner output)
   600→# ssrf_allowlist = ["172.22.0.0/16"] # CIDR ranges exempt from SSRF blocking (e.g. Docker networks)
   601→
   602→# ── Firecrawl (API-based web scraping) ────────────────────────────────────────
   603→# High-quality markdown extraction from web pages, including JS-heavy and
   604→# bot-protected sites.  Used as a standalone firecrawl_scrape tool, as a
   605→# web_search provider, and as a fallback extractor in web_fetch.
   606→# Get an API key at https://firecrawl.dev or self-host.
   607→
   608→# [tools.web.firecrawl]
   609→# enabled = false                        # Enable Firecrawl integration
   610→# api_key = "fc-..."                     # Or set FIRECRAWL_API_KEY env var
   611→# base_url = "https://api.firecrawl.dev" # API endpoint (change for self-hosted)
   612→# only_main_content = true               # Strip navs, footers, sidebars
   613→# timeout_seconds = 30                   # HTTP request timeout
   614→# cache_ttl_minutes = 15                 # Cache scraped pages (0 = no cache)
   615→# web_fetch_fallback = true              # Use as fallback when readability fails
   616→
   617→# ── Browser Automation ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   618→# Full browser control via Chrome DevTools Protocol (CDP).
   619→# Use for JavaScript-heavy sites, form filling, screenshots.
   620→
   621→[tools.browser]
   622→enabled = true                    # Enable browser tool
   623→headless = true                   # Run without visible window (true = background)
   624→viewport_width = 2560             # Default viewport width in pixels (QHD for tech users)
   625→viewport_height = 1440            # Default viewport height in pixels
   626→device_scale_factor = 2.0         # HiDPI/Retina scaling (2.0 = Retina, 1.0 = standard)
   627→max_instances = 3                 # Maximum concurrent browser instances
   628→idle_timeout_secs = 300           # Close idle browsers after this many seconds (5 min)
   629→navigation_timeout_ms = 30000     # Page load timeout in milliseconds (30 sec)
   630→sandbox = false                   # Run browser in Docker/Apple Container for isolation
   631→# container_host = "127.0.0.1"   # Host/IP to reach browser container (default: localhost)
   632→                                  # Set to "host.docker.internal" when Moltis runs inside Docker
   633→# browserless_api_version = "v1" # Browserless compatibility mode: "v1" (default) or "v2"
   634→# chrome_path = "/path/to/chrome" # Custom Chrome/Chromium binary path (auto-detected)
   635→# user_agent = "Custom UA"        # Custom user agent string
   636→# chrome_args = []                # Extra Chrome command-line arguments
   637→                                  # Example: ["--disable-extensions", "--disable-gpu"]
   638→
   639→# Domain restrictions for security.
   640→# When set, browser will refuse to navigate to domains not in this list.
   641→# This helps prevent prompt injection from untrusted websites.
   642→allowed_domains = []              # Empty = all domains allowed
   643→# allowed_domains = [
   644→#     "docs.example.com",         # Exact match
   645→#     "*.github.com",             # Wildcard: matches any subdomain of github.com
   646→#     "localhost",                # Allow localhost
   647→#     "127.0.0.1",
   648→# ]
   649→
   650→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   651→# SKILLS
   652→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   653→# Reusable prompt templates and workflows.
   654→
   655→[skills]
   656→enabled = true                    # Enable skills system
   657→search_paths = []                 # Additional directories to search for skills
   658→                                  # Default locations include ~/.moltis/skills/
   659→auto_load = []                    # Skills to always load without explicit activation
   660→                                  # Example: ["code-review", "commit"]
   661→enable_agent_sidecar_files = false # Allow agents to write supplementary text files inside personal skill dirs
   662→
   663→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   664→# MCP SERVERS
   665→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   666→# Model Context Protocol servers provide additional tools and capabilities.
   667→# See https://modelcontextprotocol.io for available servers.
   668→
   669→[mcp]
   670→request_timeout_secs = 30        # Default timeout for MCP requests
   671→# Each server has a name and configuration:
   672→#
   673→# [mcp.servers.server-name]
   674→# command = "npx"                 # Command to run (for stdio transport)
   675→# args = ["-y", "@package/name"]  # Command arguments
   676→# env = {{ KEY = "value" }}         # Environment variables for the process
   677→# enabled = true                  # Whether this server is enabled
   678→# request_timeout_secs = 90       # Optional timeout override for this server
   679→# transport = "stdio"             # Transport: "stdio" (default), "sse", or "streamable-http"
   680→# url = "http://..."              # URL for SSE/Streamable HTTP transport
   681→# headers = {{ Authorization = "Bearer ${{TOKEN}}" }}  # Optional HTTP headers for remote transport
   682→
   683→# Example: Filesystem access
   684→# [mcp.servers.filesystem]
   685→# command = "npx"
   686→# args = ["-y", "@modelcontextprotocol/server-filesystem", "/path/to/allow"]
   687→# enabled = true
   688→
   689→# Example: GitHub integration
   690→# [mcp.servers.github]
   691→# command = "npx"
   692→# args = ["-y", "@modelcontextprotocol/server-github"]
   693→# env = {{ GITHUB_TOKEN = "${{GITHUB_TOKEN}}" }}
   694→# enabled = true
   695→
   696→# Example: SSE server
   697→# [mcp.servers.remote]
   698→# transport = "sse"
   699→# url = "http://localhost:8080/mcp?api_key=$REMOTE_MCP_KEY"
   700→# headers = {{ "x-api-key" = "${{REMOTE_MCP_KEY}}" }}
   701→# enabled = true
   702→
   703→# Example: Streamable HTTP server
   704→# [mcp.servers.remote-http]
   705→# transport = "streamable-http"
   706→# url = "https://mcp.example.com/mcp"
   707→# headers = {{ Authorization = "Bearer ${{API_KEY}}" }}
   708→# enabled = true
   709→
   710→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   711→# METRICS
   712→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   713→# Prometheus metrics for observability.
   714→
   715→[metrics]
   716→enabled = true                    # Enable metrics collection
   717→prometheus_endpoint = true        # Expose /metrics endpoint for Prometheus scraping
   718→# labels = {{ environment = "production", instance = "main" }}
   719→                                  # Additional labels to add to all metrics
   720→
   721→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   722→# CRON
   723→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   724→# Settings for the cron scheduler.
   725→
   726→# [cron]
   727→# rate_limit_max = 10                # Max jobs created per rate limit window
   728→# rate_limit_window_secs = 60        # Rate limit window in seconds
   729→# session_retention_days = 7         # Auto-clean cron sessions older than N days (0 or omit to disable)
   730→# auto_prune_cron_containers = true  # Auto-remove sandbox containers after cron job completion
   731→
   732→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   733→# HEARTBEAT
   734→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   735→# Periodic health-check agent turns to keep the agent "alive" and responsive.
   736→
   737→[heartbeat]
   738→enabled = true                    # Enable periodic heartbeats
   739→every = "30m"                     # Interval between heartbeats (e.g., "30m", "1h", "6h")
   740→# model = "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-20250514"  # Override model for heartbeats
   741→# prompt = "..."                  # Custom heartbeat prompt (default: built-in)
   742→ack_max_chars = 300               # Max characters for acknowledgment reply
   743→deliver = false                   # Deliver heartbeat replies to a channel account
   744→# channel = "my-bot"              # Channel account identifier (required when deliver = true)
   745→# to = "123456789"                # Chat/recipient ID (required when deliver = true)
   746→sandbox_enabled = true            # Run heartbeat commands in sandbox
   747→# sandbox_image = "..."           # Override sandbox image for heartbeats
   748→
   749→# Active hours window - heartbeats only run during this time
   750→[heartbeat.active_hours]
   751→start = "08:00"                   # Start time (HH:MM, 24-hour format)
   752→end = "24:00"                     # End time (HH:MM, "24:00" = end of day)
   753→timezone = "local"                # Timezone: "local" or IANA name like "Europe/Paris"
   754→
   755→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   756→# FAILOVER
   757→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   758→# Automatic fallback to alternative models/providers on failure.
   759→
   760→[failover]
   761→enabled = true                    # Enable automatic failover
   762→fallback_models = []              # Ordered list of fallback models
   763→                                  # Empty = auto-build chain from all registered models
   764→                                  # Example: ["openai/gpt-4o", "anthropic/claude-3-haiku"]
   765→
   766→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   767→# VOICE
   768→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   769→# Voice provider settings for text-to-speech (TTS) and speech-to-text (STT).
   770→# `providers` controls what appears in the Settings UI provider list.
   771→
   772→[voice.tts]
   773→enabled = true                    # Enable text-to-speech
   774→# provider = "openai"             # Active TTS provider (auto-selects first configured if omitted)
   775→providers = ["openai", "elevenlabs"] # UI allowlist (empty = show all TTS providers)
   776→# All available TTS providers:
   777→#   "openai", "elevenlabs", "google", "piper", "coqui"
   778→
   779→[voice.stt]
   780→enabled = true                    # Enable speech-to-text
   781→# provider = "whisper"            # Active STT provider (auto-selects first configured if omitted)
   782→providers = ["whisper", "mistral", "elevenlabs"] # UI allowlist (empty = show all STT providers)
   783→# All available STT providers:
   784→#   "whisper", "groq", "deepgram", "google", "mistral",
   785→#   "voxtral-local", "whisper-cli", "sherpa-onnx", "elevenlabs-stt"
   786→
   787→# No api_key needed for OpenAI TTS/Whisper when OpenAI is configured as an LLM provider.
   788→# [voice.tts.openai]
   789→# base_url = "https://api.openai.com/v1"     # API endpoint (change for Chatterbox, etc.)
   790→# voice = "alloy"                 # alloy, echo, fable, onyx, nova, shimmer
   791→# model = "tts-1"                 # tts-1 or tts-1-hd
   792→
   793→# [voice.stt.whisper]
   794→# model = "whisper-1"             # default; override when using an OpenAI-compatible proxy
   795→# language = "en"                 # Optional ISO 639-1 hint; omit for auto-detect
   796→
   797→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   798→# NGROK
   799→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   800→# Expose moltis through a public HTTPS tunnel managed by ngrok.
   801→# Requires a build with the `ngrok` feature and an ngrok authtoken.
   802→
   803→[ngrok]
   804→enabled = false                   # true = create a public HTTPS tunnel at startup
   805→# authtoken = "${{NGROK_AUTHTOKEN}}" # Optional if NGROK_AUTHTOKEN env var is already set
   806→# domain = "team-gateway.ngrok.app"  # Optional reserved/static ngrok domain
   807→
   808→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   809→# TAILSCALE
   810→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   811→# Expose moltis via Tailscale Serve (private) or Funnel (public).
   812→
   813→[tailscale]
   814→mode = "off"                      # Tailscale mode:
   815→                                  #   "off"    - Disabled
   816→                                  #   "serve"  - Tailnet-only HTTPS (private)
   817→                                  #   "funnel" - Public HTTPS via Tailscale
   818→reset_on_exit = true              # Reset serve/funnel when gateway shuts down
   819→
   820→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   821→# MEMORY / EMBEDDINGS
   822→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   823→# Configure the embedding provider for memory/RAG features.
   824→
   825→[memory]
   826→# style = "hybrid"               # High-level memory behavior (default = "hybrid"):
   827→                                  #   "hybrid"      - Prompt MEMORY.md + memory_search/memory_get/memory_save
   828→                                  #   "prompt-only" - Prompt MEMORY.md only, no memory tools
   829→                                  #   "search-only" - Memory tools only, no prompt MEMORY.md injection
   830→                                  #   "off"         - Disable prompt memory injection and memory tools
   831→#                                  # Prompt freshness is configured separately in [chat].prompt_memory_mode
   832→# agent_write_mode = "hybrid"    # Where agent-authored memory writes can land (default = "hybrid"):
   833→                                  #   "hybrid"      - Allow MEMORY.md and memory/<name>.md
   834→                                  #   "prompt-only" - Allow MEMORY.md only
   835→                                  #   "search-only" - Allow memory/<name>.md only
   836→                                  #   "off"         - Disable agent-authored memory writes
   837→# user_profile_write_mode = "explicit-and-auto"  # How Moltis writes USER.md (default = "explicit-and-auto"):
   838→                                  #   "explicit-and-auto" - Settings saves plus silent timezone/location capture
   839→                                  #   "explicit-only"     - Settings saves only, no silent timezone/location capture
   840→                                  #   "off"               - Do not write USER.md, keep profile in moltis.toml only
   841→# backend = "builtin"            # Memory backend (default = "builtin"):
   842→                                  #   "builtin" - Built-in SQLite indexer and retriever
   843→                                  #   "qmd"     - External QMD CLI backend
   844→# provider = "auto"               # Embedding provider for the built-in backend (default = auto-detect):
   845→                                  #   "local"   - Built-in local embeddings
   846→                                  #   "ollama"  - Ollama server
   847→                                  #   "openai"  - OpenAI API
   848→                                  #   "custom"  - Custom endpoint
   849→                                  #   (none)    - Auto-detect from available providers
   850→                                  # Ignored while backend = "qmd"
   851→# disable_rag = false             # true => keyword-only search, disables embedding retrieval
   852→# citations = "auto"              # Citation mode (default = "auto"): "on", "off", or "auto"
   853→# llm_reranking = false           # Use the LLM to rerank search results (only meaningful when RAG is enabled)
   854→# search_merge_strategy = "rrf"   # Merge strategy (default = "rrf"): "rrf" or "linear"
   855→# session_export = "on-new-or-reset"  # Session transcript export policy (default = "on-new-or-reset"):
   856→                                  #   "on-new-or-reset" - Export transcripts on /new and /reset
   857→                                  #   "off"             - Disable session transcript export
   858→#                                  # Exported transcripts become searchable memory, not prompt memory
   859→# base_url = "http://localhost:11434/v1"  # Builtin backend only: embedding API base (host, /v1, or /embeddings)
   860→# model = "nomic-embed-text"      # Builtin backend only: embedding model name
   861→# api_key = "..."                 # Builtin backend only: API key (optional for local endpoints like Ollama)
   862→
   863→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   864→# CHANNELS
   865→# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   866→# External messaging integrations.
   867→# Note: channels added or edited in the web UI are stored in Moltis's internal
   868→# database at data_dir()/moltis.db. They are not written back into this file.
   869→# Keep channel config here only if you want to manage it manually in TOML.
   870→
   871→[channels]
   872→# Which channel types appear in the web UI's "+ Add Channel" menu.
   873→# Default: ["telegram", "msteams", "discord", "slack", "matrix", "nostr"]
   874→# Add "whatsapp" to enable it in the UI.
   875→# offered = ["telegram", "msteams", "discord", "slack", "matrix", "nostr", "whatsapp"]
   876→
   877→# WhatsApp linked-device accounts
   878→# [channels.whatsapp.my-bot]
   879→# dm_policy = "open"              # "open", "allowlist", or "disabled"
   880→# group_policy = "disabled"       # "open", "allowlist", or "disabled"
   881→# mention_mode = "always"         # "always", "mention", or "none"
   882→# model = "anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-20250514"
   883→# model_provider = "anthropic"
   884→# agent_id = "support"            # Default agent for this account
   885→# otp_self_approval = true        # OTP self-approval for non-allowlisted DM users
   886→# otp_cooldown_secs = 300         # Cooldown after 3 failed OTP attempts
   887→# [channels.whatsapp.my-bot.channel_overrides."120363456789@g.us"]
   888→# agent_id = "triage"
   889→# [channels.whatsapp.my-bot.user_overrides."15551234567@s.whatsapp.net"]
   890→# agent_id = "research"
   891→
   892→# Telegram bots
   893→# [channels.telegram.my-bot]
   894→# token = "..."                   # Bot token from @BotFather
   895→# dm_policy = "allowlist"         # "open", "allowlist", or "disabled"
   896→# group_policy = "open"           # "open", "allowlist", or "disabled"
   897→# mention_mode = "mention"        # "mention", "always", or "none"
   898→# allowlist = []                  # Telegram user IDs or usernames (strings)
   899→# group_allowlist = []            # Telegram group/chat IDs (strings)
   900→# reply_to_message = false        # Send responses as Telegram replies
   901→# agent_id = "research"           # Default agent for this bot
   902→# otp_self_approval = true        # OTP self-approval for non-allowlisted DM users
   903→# otp_cooldown_secs = 300         # Cooldown after 3 failed OTP attempts
   904→# stream_mode = "edit_in_place"   # "edit_in_place" or "off"
   905→# edit_throttle_ms = 300          # Min ms between streaming edits
   906→# [channels.telegram.my-bot.channel_overrides."-1001234567890"]
   907→# agent_id = "triage"
   908→# [channels.telegram.my-bot.user_overrides."123456789"]
   909→# agent_id = "research"
   910→#
   911→# Per-channel tool policy (restrict tools by chat type and sender):
   912→# [channels.telegram.my-bot.tools.groups.group]
   913→# deny = ["exec", "browser"]      # Deny dangerous tools in group chats
   914→# [channels.telegram.my-bot.tools.groups.group.by_sender."123456"]
   915→# allow = ["*"]                    # Override: trusted sender gets all tools
   916→# [channels.telegram.my-bot.tools.groups.private]
   917→# allow = []                       # DMs: allow all tools (default)
   918→
   919→# Microsoft Teams bots
   920→# [channels.msteams.my-bot]
   921→# app_id = "..."                  # Azure Bot App ID
   922→# app_password = "..."            # Azure Bot App Password (client secret)
   923→# tenant_id = "botframework.com"  # Azure AD tenant ID (for JWT validation)
   924→# webhook_secret = "..."          # Optional query secret for webhook URL (?secret=...)
   925→# allowlist = []                  # User IDs allowed to DM (empty = all unless dm_policy=allowlist)
   926→# dm_policy = "allowlist"         # "open", "allowlist", or "disabled"
   927→# group_policy = "open"           # "open", "allowlist", or "disabled"
   928→# mention_mode = "mention"        # "mention", "always", or "none"
   929→# stream_mode = "edit_in_place"   # "edit_in_place" or "off"
   930→# edit_throttle_ms = 1500         # Min ms between streaming edits
   931→# text_chunk_limit = 4000         # Max chars per message chunk
   932→# reply_style = "top_level"       # "top_level" or "thread"
   933→# welcome_card = true             # Show welcome card in DMs
   934→# group_welcome_card = false      # Show welcome text in group chats
   935→# bot_name = "Moltis"             # Bot display name for welcome cards
   936→# prompt_starters = []            # Prompt starter buttons on welcome card
   937→# max_retries = 3                 # Max retry attempts for failed sends
   938→# history_limit = 50              # Max messages for thread context (Graph API)
   939→
   940→# Discord bots
   941→# [channels.discord.my-bot]
   942→# token = "..."                   # Bot token from Discord Developer Portal
   943→# dm_policy = "allowlist"         # "open", "allowlist", or "disabled"
   944→# group_policy = "open"           # "open", "allowlist", or "disabled"
   945→# mention_mode = "mention"        # "mention", "always", or "none"
   946→# allowlist = []                  # Discord user IDs allowed to DM
   947→# guild_allowlist = []            # Discord guild/server IDs (empty = all)
   948→# agent_id = "research"           # Default agent for this bot
   949→# reply_to_message = false        # Send responses as Discord replies
   950→# ack_reaction = "👀"             # Emoji reaction while processing (omit to disable)
   951→# activity = "with AI"            # Bot activity status text
   952→# activity_type = "custom"        # "playing", "listening", "watching", "competing", or "custom"
   953→# status = "online"               # "online", "idle", "dnd", or "invisible"
   954→# otp_self_approval = true        # OTP self-approval for non-allowlisted DM users
   955→# otp_cooldown_secs = 300         # Cooldown after 3 failed OTP attempts
   956→
   957→# Slack bots
   958→# [channels.slack.my-bot]
   959→# bot_token = "xoxb-..."          # Bot user OAuth token
   960→# app_token = "xapp-..."          # App-level token for Socket Mode
   961→# connection_mode = "socket_mode" # "socket_mode" or "events_api"
   962→# signing_secret = "..."          # Required for events_api mode
   963→# dm_policy = "allowlist"         # "open", "allowlist", or "disabled"
   964→# group_policy = "open"           # "open", "allowlist", or "disabled"
   965→# mention_mode = "mention"        # "mention", "always", or "none"
   966→# allowlist = []                  # Slack user IDs (strings)
   967→# channel_allowlist = []          # Slack channel IDs (strings)
   968→# stream_mode = "edit_in_place"   # "edit_in_place", "native", or "off"
   969→# edit_throttle_ms = 500          # Min ms between streaming edits
   970→# thread_replies = true           # Reply in threads
   971→
   972→# Matrix bots / appservices using OIDC, access tokens, or password login
   973→# Three authentication modes:
   974→#   oidc         - OAuth 2.0/OIDC via Matrix Authentication Service (MSC3861).
   975→#                  Recommended for modern homeservers (e.g. matrix.org since April 2025).
   976→#                  Authenticates via browser; no password or token needed.
   977→#   password     - UIAA password login. Required for encrypted Matrix chats on
   978→#                  older homeservers. Moltis creates and persists its own device keys.
   979→#   access_token - Reuses an existing Matrix session. Plain traffic only (no E2EE).
   980→# [channels.matrix.my-bot]
   981→# homeserver = "https://matrix.example.com"
   982→# auth_mode = "oidc"              # "oidc", "password", or "access_token" (auto-detected if omitted)
   983→# access_token = "syt_..."        # Required for access_token mode
   984→# password = "..."                # Required for password mode
   985→# user_id = "@bot:example.com"    # Required for password login, auto-detected for token/OIDC auth
   986→# device_id = "MOLTISBOT"         # Optional device ID for session restore
   987→# device_display_name = "Moltis Matrix Bot"  # Optional display name for password logins
   988→# ownership_mode = "moltis_owned" # "moltis_owned" or "user_managed"
   989→# dm_policy = "allowlist"         # "open", "allowlist", or "disabled"
   990→# room_policy = "allowlist"       # "open", "allowlist", or "disabled"
   991→# mention_mode = "mention"        # "mention", "always", or "none"
   992→# room_allowlist = []             # Matrix room IDs or aliases
   993→# user_allowlist = []             # Matrix user IDs
   994→# auto_join = "always"            # "always", "allowlist", or "off"
   995→# model = "gpt-4.1"
   996→# model_provider = "openai"
   997→# stream_mode = "edit_in_place"   # "edit_in_place" or "off"
   998→# edit_throttle_ms = 500          # Min ms between streaming edits
   999→# stream_min_initial_chars = 30   # Delay first streamed send until this many chars
  1000→# reply_to_message = true         # Send threaded/rich replies when possible
  1001→# ack_reaction = "👀"             # Emoji reaction while processing (omit to disable)
  1002→# otp_self_approval = true        # OTP self-approval for non-allowlisted DM users
  1003→# otp_cooldown_secs = 300         # Cooldown after 3 failed OTP attempts
  1004→
  1005→# Nostr DM bots (NIP-04 encrypted direct messages)
  1006→# [channels.nostr.my-bot]
  1007→# secret_key = "nsec1..."          # Secret key (nsec1 bech32 or 64-char hex)
```

```
    11→/// Load config from the given path (any supported format).
    12→///
    13→/// After parsing, `MOLTIS_*` env vars are applied as overrides.
    14→pub fn load_config(path: &Path) -> crate::Result<MoltisConfig> {
    15→    let raw = std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|source| {
    16→        crate::Error::external(format!("failed to read {}", path.display()), source)
    17→    })?;
    18→    let raw = substitute_env(&raw);
    19→    let config = parse_config(&raw, path)?;
    20→    Ok(apply_env_overrides(config))
    21→}

    24→pub fn load_config_value(path: &Path) -> crate::Result<serde_json::Value> {
    25→    let raw = std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|source| {
    26→        crate::Error::external(format!("failed to read {}", path.display()), source)
    27→    })?;

    31→
    32→/// Discover and load config from standard locations.
    33→///
    34→/// Search order:
    35→/// 1. `./moltis.{toml,yaml,yml,json}` (project-local)
    36→/// 2. `~/.config/moltis/moltis.{toml,yaml,yml,json}` (user-global)
    37→///
    38→/// Returns `MoltisConfig::default()` if no config file is found.
    39→///
    40→/// If the config has port 0 (either from defaults or missing `[server]` section),
    41→/// a random available port is generated and saved to the config file.
    42→pub fn discover_and_load() -> MoltisConfig {
    43→    let mut cfg = if let Some(path) = find_config_file() {
    44→        debug!(path = %path.display(), "loading config");
    45→        match load_config(&path) {
    46→            Ok(mut cfg) => {
    47→                // If port is 0 (default/missing), generate a random port and save it.
    48→                // Use `save_config_to_path` directly instead of `save_config` because
    49→                // this function may be called from within `update_config`, which already
    50→                // holds `CONFIG_SAVE_LOCK`. Re-acquiring a `std::sync::Mutex` on the
    51→                // same thread would deadlock.
    52→                if cfg.server.port == 0 {
    53→                    cfg.server.port = generate_random_port();
    54→                    debug!(
    55→                        port = cfg.server.port,
    56→                        "generated random port for existing config"
    57→                    );
    58→                    if let Err(e) = save_config_to_path(&path, &cfg) {
    59→                        warn!(error = %e, "failed to save config with generated port");
    60→                    }
    61→                }
    62→                cfg // env overrides already applied by load_config
    63→            },
    64→            Err(e) => {
    65→                warn!(path = %path.display(), error = %e, "failed to load config, using defaults");
    66→                apply_env_overrides(MoltisConfig::default())
    67→            },
    68→        }
    69→    } else {
    70→        let default_path = find_or_default_config_path();
    71→        debug!(
    72→            path = %default_path.display(),
    73→            "no config file found, writing default config with random port"
    74→        );
    75→        let mut config = MoltisConfig::default();
    76→        // Generate a unique port for this installation
    77→        config.server.port = generate_random_port();
    78→        if let Err(e) = write_default_config(&default_path, &config) {
    79→            warn!(
    80→                path = %default_path.display(),
    81→                error = %e,
    82→                "failed to write default config file, continuing with in-memory defaults"
    83→            );
    84→        } else {
    85→            info!(
    86→                path = %default_path.display(),
    87→                "wrote default config template"
    88→            );
    89→        }
    90→        apply_env_overrides(config)
    91→    };
    92→
    93→    // Merge markdown agent definitions (TOML presets take precedence).
    94→    let agent_defs = crate::agent_defs::discover_agent_defs();
    95→    if !agent_defs.is_empty() {
    96→        debug!(
    97→            count = agent_defs.len(),
    98→            "discovered markdown agent definitions"
    99→        );
   100→        crate::agent_defs::merge_agent_defs(&mut cfg.agents.presets, agent_defs);
   101→    }
   102→
   103→    cfg
   104→}
   105→
   106→/// Find the first config file in standard locations.
   107→///
   108→/// When a config dir override is set, only that directory is searched —
   109→/// project-local and user-global paths are skipped for isolation.
   110→pub fn find_config_file() -> Option<PathBuf> {
   111→    if let Some(dir) = config_dir_override() {
   112→        for name in CONFIG_FILENAMES {
   113→            let p = dir.join(name);
   114→            if p.exists() {
   115→                return Some(p);
   116→            }
   117→        }
   118→        // Override is set — don't fall through to other locations.
   119→        return None;
   120→    }
   121→
   122→    // Project-local
   123→    for name in CONFIG_FILENAMES {
   124→        let p = PathBuf::from(name);
   125→        if p.exists() {
   126→            return Some(p);
   127→        }
   128→    }
   129→
   130→    // User-global: ~/.config/moltis/
   131→    if let Some(dir) = home_dir().map(|h| h.join(".config").join("moltis")) {
   132→        for name in CONFIG_FILENAMES {
   133→            let p = dir.join(name);
   134→            if p.exists() {
   135→                return Some(p);
   136→            }
   137→        }
   138→    }
   139→
   140→    None
   141→}
   142→
   143→pub fn find_or_default_config_path() -> PathBuf {
   144→    if let Some(path) = find_config_file() {
   145→        return path;
   146→    }
   147→    config_dir()
   148→        .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("."))
   149→        .join("moltis.toml")
   150→}
   151→
   152→/// Lock guarding config read-modify-write cycles.
   153→struct ConfigSaveState {
   154→    target_path: Option<PathBuf>,
   155→}
   156→
   157→/// Lock guarding config read-modify-write cycles and the target config path
   158→/// being synchronized.
   159→static CONFIG_SAVE_LOCK: Mutex<ConfigSaveState> = Mutex::new(ConfigSaveState { target_path: None });
   160→
   161→/// Atomically load the current config, apply `f`, and save.
   162→///
   163→/// Acquires a process-wide lock so concurrent callers cannot race.
   164→/// Returns the path written to.
   165→pub fn update_config(f: impl FnOnce(&mut MoltisConfig)) -> crate::Result<PathBuf> {
   166→    let mut guard = CONFIG_SAVE_LOCK.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());
   167→    let target_path = find_or_default_config_path();
   168→    guard.target_path = Some(target_path.clone());
   169→    let mut config = discover_and_load();
   170→    f(&mut config);
   171→    save_config_to_path(&target_path, &config)
   172→}
   173→
   174→/// Serialize `config` to TOML and write it to the user-global config path.
   175→///
   176→/// Creates parent directories if needed. Returns the path written to.
   177→///
   178→/// Prefer [`update_config`] for read-modify-write cycles to avoid races.
   179→pub fn save_config(config: &MoltisConfig) -> crate::Result<PathBuf> {
   180→    let mut guard = CONFIG_SAVE_LOCK.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());
   181→    let target_path = find_or_default_config_path();
   182→    guard.target_path = Some(target_path.clone());
   183→    save_config_to_path(&target_path, config)
   184→}
   185→
   186→/// Write raw TOML to the config file, preserving comments.
   187→///
   188→/// Validates the input by parsing it first. Acquires the config save lock
   189→/// so concurrent callers cannot race.  Returns the path written to.
   190→pub fn save_raw_config(toml_str: &str) -> crate::Result<PathBuf> {
   191→    let _: MoltisConfig = toml::from_str(toml_str)
   192→        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::external(format!("invalid config: {source}"), source))?;
   193→    let mut guard = CONFIG_SAVE_LOCK.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner());
   194→    let path = find_or_default_config_path();
   195→    guard.target_path = Some(path.clone());
   196→    if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
   197→        std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
   198→    }
   199→    std::fs::write(&path, toml_str)?;
   200→    debug!(path = %path.display(), "saved raw config");
   201→    Ok(path)
   202→}
   203→
   204→/// Serialize `config` to TOML and write it to the provided path.
   205→///
   206→/// For existing TOML files, this preserves user comments by merging the new
   207→/// serialized values into the current document structure before writing.
   208→pub fn save_config_to_path(path: &Path, config: &MoltisConfig) -> crate::Result<PathBuf> {
   209→    if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
   210→        std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
   211→    }
   212→    let toml_str = toml::to_string_pretty(config)
   213→        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::external("serialize config", source))?;
   214→
   215→    let is_toml_path = path
   216→        .extension()
   217→        .and_then(|ext| ext.to_str())
   218→        .is_some_and(|ext| ext.eq_ignore_ascii_case("toml"));
   219→
   220→    if is_toml_path && path.exists() {
   221→        if let Err(error) = merge_toml_preserving_comments(path, &toml_str) {
   222→            warn!(
   223→                path = %path.display(),
   224→                error = %error,
   225→                "failed to preserve TOML comments, rewriting config without comments"
   226→            );
   227→            std::fs::write(path, toml_str)?;
   228→        }
   229→    } else {
   230→        std::fs::write(path, toml_str)?;
   231→    }
   232→
   233→    debug!(path = %path.display(), "saved config");
   234→    Ok(path.to_path_buf())
   235→}
   236→
   237→fn merge_toml_preserving_comments(path: &Path, updated_toml: &str) -> crate::Result<()> {
   238→    let current_toml = std::fs::read_to_string(path)?;
   239→    let mut current_doc = current_toml
   240→        .parse::<toml_edit::DocumentMut>()
   241→        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::external("parse existing TOML", source))?;
   242→    let updated_doc = updated_toml
   243→        .parse::<toml_edit::DocumentMut>()
   244→        .map_err(|source| crate::Error::external("parse updated TOML", source))?;
   245→
   246→    merge_toml_tables(current_doc.as_table_mut(), updated_doc.as_table());
   247→    std::fs::write(path, current_doc.to_string())?;
   248→    Ok(())
   249→}
   250→
   251→pub(super) fn merge_toml_tables(current: &mut toml_edit::Table, updated: &toml_edit::Table) {
   252→    let current_keys: Vec<String> = current.iter().map(|(key, _)| key.to_string()).collect();
   253→    for key in current_keys {
   254→        if !updated.contains_key(&key) {
   255→            let _ = current.remove(&key);
   256→        }
   257→    }
   258→
   259→    for (key, updated_item) in updated.iter() {
   260→        if let Some(current_item) = current.get_mut(key) {
   261→            merge_toml_items(current_item, updated_item);
   262→        } else {
   263→            // Clone the item and strip `doc_position` metadata inherited from
   264→            // the source document.  Without this, toml_edit uses the position
   265→            // from the *serialized* document, causing new sub-tables to be
   266→            // interleaved among existing sections instead of appearing after
   267→            // their parent (GH-684).
   268→            current.insert(key, clone_item_without_positions(updated_item));
   269→        }
   270→    }
   271→}
   272→
   273→/// Deep-clone a `toml_edit::Item`, stripping `doc_position` from every table
   274→/// so that newly inserted entries get auto-positioned by `toml_edit` rather
   275→/// than inheriting stale positions from a different document.
   276→fn clone_item_without_positions(item: &toml_edit::Item) -> toml_edit::Item {
   277→    match item {
   278→        toml_edit::Item::Table(t) => toml_edit::Item::Table(clone_table_without_positions(t)),
   279→        toml_edit::Item::ArrayOfTables(arr) => {
   280→            let mut new_arr = toml_edit::ArrayOfTables::new();
   281→            for table in arr.iter() {
   282→                new_arr.push(clone_table_without_positions(table));
   283→            }
   284→            toml_edit::Item::ArrayOfTables(new_arr)
   285→        },
   286→        other => other.clone(),
   287→    }
   288→}
   289→
   290→/// Clone a table, recursively stripping `doc_position` so new tables get
   291→/// auto-positioned when inserted into a different document.
   292→fn clone_table_without_positions(src: &toml_edit::Table) -> toml_edit::Table {
   293→    let mut dst = toml_edit::Table::new();
   294→    // doc_position is None for manually created tables → auto-positioned
   295→    dst.set_implicit(src.is_implicit());
   296→    dst.set_dotted(src.is_dotted());
   297→    *dst.decor_mut() = src.decor().clone();
   298→    for (key, item) in src.iter() {
   299→        dst.insert(key, clone_item_without_positions(item));
   300→        // Preserve key decorations (whitespace/comments around the key)
   301→        if let (Some(src_key), Some(mut dst_key)) = (src.key(key), dst.key_mut(key)) {
   302→            *dst_key.leaf_decor_mut() = src_key.leaf_decor().clone();
   303→            *dst_key.dotted_decor_mut() = src_key.dotted_decor().clone();
   304→        }
   305→    }
   306→    dst
   307→}
   308→
   309→fn merge_toml_items(current: &mut toml_edit::Item, updated: &toml_edit::Item) {
   310→    match (current, updated) {
   311→        (toml_edit::Item::Table(current_table), toml_edit::Item::Table(updated_table)) => {
   312→            merge_toml_tables(current_table, updated_table);
   313→        },
   314→        (toml_edit::Item::Value(current_value), toml_edit::Item::Value(updated_value)) => {
   315→            let existing_decor = current_value.decor().clone();
   316→            *current_value = updated_value.clone();
   317→            *current_value.decor_mut() = existing_decor;
   318→        },
   319→        (current_item, updated_item) => {
   320→            *current_item = updated_item.clone();
   321→        },
   322→    }
   323→}
   324→
   325→/// Write the default config file to the user-global config path.
   326→/// Only called when no config file exists yet.
   327→/// Uses a comprehensive template with all options documented.
   328→pub(super) fn write_default_config(path: &Path, config: &MoltisConfig) -> crate::Result<()> {
   329→    if path.exists() {
   330→        return Ok(());
   331→    }
   332→    if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
   333→        std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
   334→    }
   335→    // Use the documented template instead of plain serialization
   336→    let toml_str = crate::template::default_config_template(config.server.port);
   337→    std::fs::write(path, &toml_str)?;
   338→    debug!(path = %path.display(), "wrote default config file with template");
   339→    Ok(())
   340→}
   341→
   342→/// Apply `MOLTIS_*` environment variable overrides to a loaded config.
   343→///
   344→/// Maps env vars to config fields using `__` as a section separator and
   345→/// lowercasing. For example:
   346→/// - `MOLTIS_AUTH_DISABLED=true` → `auth.disabled = true`
   347→/// - `MOLTIS_TOOLS_EXEC_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_SECS=60` → `tools.exec.default_timeout_secs = 60`
   348→/// - `MOLTIS_CHAT_MESSAGE_QUEUE_MODE=collect` → `chat.message_queue_mode = "collect"`
   349→///
   350→/// The config is serialized to a JSON value, env overrides are merged in,
   351→/// then deserialized back. Only env vars with the `MOLTIS_` prefix are
   352→/// considered. `MOLTIS_CONFIG_DIR`, `MOLTIS_DATA_DIR`, `MOLTIS_SHARE_DIR`,
   353→/// `MOLTIS_ASSETS_DIR`, `MOLTIS_TOKEN`, `MOLTIS_PASSWORD`, `MOLTIS_TAILSCALE`,
   354→/// `MOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_RP_ID`, and `MOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_ORIGIN` are excluded
   355→/// (they are handled separately).
   356→pub fn apply_env_overrides(config: MoltisConfig) -> MoltisConfig {
   357→    apply_env_overrides_with(config, std::env::vars())
   358→}
   359→
   360→/// Apply env overrides from an arbitrary iterator of (key, value) pairs.
   361→/// Exposed for testing without mutating the process environment.
   362→pub(super) fn apply_env_overrides_with(
   363→    config: MoltisConfig,
   364→    vars: impl Iterator<Item = (String, String)>,
   365→) -> MoltisConfig {
   366→    use serde_json::Value;
   367→
   368→    const EXCLUDED: &[&str] = &[
   369→        "MOLTIS_CONFIG_DIR",
   370→        "MOLTIS_DATA_DIR",
   371→        "MOLTIS_SHARE_DIR",
   372→        "MOLTIS_ASSETS_DIR",
   373→        "MOLTIS_TOKEN",
   374→        "MOLTIS_PASSWORD",
   375→        "MOLTIS_TAILSCALE",
   376→        "MOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_RP_ID",
   377→        "MOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_ORIGIN",
   378→    ];
   379→
   380→    let mut root: Value = match serde_json::to_value(&config) {
   381→        Ok(v) => v,
   382→        Err(e) => {
   383→            warn!(error = %e, "failed to serialize config for env override");
   384→            return config;
   385→        },
   386→    };
   387→
   388→    for (key, val) in vars {
   389→        if !key.starts_with("MOLTIS_") {
   390→            continue;
   391→        }
   392→        if EXCLUDED.contains(&key.as_str()) {
   393→            continue;
   394→        }
   395→
   396→        // MOLTIS_AUTH__DISABLED → ["auth", "disabled"]
   397→        let path_parts: Vec<String> = key["MOLTIS_".len()..]
   398→            .split("__")
   399→            .map(|segment| segment.to_lowercase())
   400→            .collect();
   401→
   402→        if path_parts.is_empty() {
   403→            continue;
   404→        }
   405→
   406→        // Navigate to the parent object and set the leaf value.
   407→        let parsed_val = parse_env_value(&val);
   408→        set_nested(&mut root, &path_parts, parsed_val);
   409→    }
   410→
   411→    match serde_json::from_value(root) {
   412→        Ok(cfg) => cfg,
   413→        Err(e) => {
   414→            warn!(error = %e, "failed to apply env overrides, using config as-is");
   415→            config
   416→        },
   417→    }
   418→}
   419→
   420→/// Parse a string env value into a JSON value, trying bool and number first.
   421→pub(super) fn parse_env_value(val: &str) -> serde_json::Value {
   422→    let trimmed = val.trim();
   423→
   424→    // Support JSON arrays/objects for list-like env overrides, e.g.
   425→    // MOLTIS_PROVIDERS__OFFERED='["openai","github-copilot"]' or '[]'.
   426→    if ((trimmed.starts_with('[') && trimmed.ends_with(']'))
   427→        || (trimmed.starts_with('{') && trimmed.ends_with('}')))
   428→        && let Ok(parsed) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(trimmed)
   429→    {
   430→        return parsed;
   431→    }
   432→
   433→    if val.eq_ignore_ascii_case("true") {
   434→        return serde_json::Value::Bool(true);
   435→    }
   436→    if val.eq_ignore_ascii_case("false") {
   437→        return serde_json::Value::Bool(false);
   438→    }
   439→    if let Ok(n) = val.parse::<i64>() {
   440→        return serde_json::Value::Number(n.into());
   441→    }
   442→    if let Ok(n) = val.parse::<f64>()
   443→        && let Some(n) = serde_json::Number::from_f64(n)
   444→    {
   445→        return serde_json::Value::Number(n);
   446→    }
   447→    serde_json::Value::String(val.to_string())
   448→}
   449→
   450→/// Set a value at a nested JSON path, creating intermediate objects as needed.
   451→pub(super) fn set_nested(root: &mut serde_json::Value, path: &[String], val: serde_json::Value) {
   452→    if path.is_empty() {
   453→        return;
   454→    }
   455→    let mut current = root;
   456→    for (i, key) in path.iter().enumerate() {
   457→        if i == path.len() - 1 {
   458→            if let serde_json::Value::Object(map) = current {
   459→                map.insert(key.clone(), val);
   460→            }
   461→            return;
   462→        }
   463→        if !current.get(key).is_some_and(|v| v.is_object())
   464→            && let serde_json::Value::Object(map) = current
   465→        {
   466→            map.insert(key.clone(), serde_json::Value::Object(Default::default()));
   467→        }
   468→        let Some(next) = current.get_mut(key) else {
   469→            return;
   470→        };
   471→        current = next;
   472→    }
   473→}
   474→
   475→pub(super) fn parse_config(raw: &str, path: &Path) -> crate::Result<MoltisConfig> {
   476→    let ext = path.extension().and_then(|e| e.to_str()).unwrap_or("toml");
   477→
   478→    match ext {
   479→        "toml" => Ok(toml::from_str(raw)?),
   480→        "yaml" | "yml" => Ok(serde_yaml::from_str(raw)?),
   481→        "json" => Ok(serde_json::from_str(raw)?),
   482→        _ => Err(crate::Error::message(format!(
   483→            "unsupported config format: .{ext}"
   484→        ))),
   485→    }
   486→}
   487→
   488→fn parse_config_value(raw: &str, path: &Path) -> crate::Result<serde_json::Value> {
   489→    let ext = path.extension().and_then(|e| e.to_str()).unwrap_or("toml");
   490→
   491→    match ext {
   492→        "toml" => {
   493→            let v: toml::Value = toml::from_str(raw)?;
   494→            Ok(serde_json::to_value(v)?)
   495→        },
   496→        "yaml" | "yml" => {
   497→            let v: serde_yaml::Value = serde_yaml::from_str(raw)?;
   498→            Ok(serde_json::to_value(v)?)
   499→        },
   500→        "json" => Ok(serde_json::from_str(raw)?),
   501→        _ => Err(crate::Error::message(format!(
   502→            "unsupported config format: .{ext}"
   503→        ))),
   504→    }
   505→}
   506→
```
