     1→# Security Architecture
     2→
     3→Moltis is designed with a defense-in-depth security model. This document
     4→explains the key security features and provides guidance for production
     5→deployments.
     6→
     7→## Overview
     8→
     9→Moltis runs AI agents that can execute code and interact with external systems.
    10→This power requires multiple layers of protection:
    11→
    12→1. **Human-in-the-loop approval** for dangerous commands
    13→2. **Sandbox isolation** for command execution
    14→3. **Channel authorization** for external integrations
    15→4. **Rate limiting** to prevent resource abuse
    16→5. **Scope-based access control** for API authorization
    17→
    18→For marketplace-style skill/plugin hardening (trust gating, provenance pinning,
    19→drift re-trust, dependency install guards, kill switch, audit log), see
    20→[Third-Party Skills Security](skills-security.md).
    21→
    22→## Command Execution Approval
    23→
    24→By default, Moltis requires explicit user approval before executing potentially
    25→dangerous commands. This "human-in-the-loop" design ensures the AI cannot take
    26→destructive actions without consent.
    27→
    28→### How It Works
    29→
    30→When the agent wants to run a command:
    31→
    32→1. The command is analyzed against approval policies
    33→2. If approval is required, the user sees a prompt in the UI. Channel-backed
    34→   sessions also receive a notification in the originating channel so the run
    35→   does not stall silently.
    36→3. The user can approve, deny, or modify the command
    37→4. Only approved commands execute
    38→
    39→For channel-backed sessions, operators can also use `/approvals` to list the
    40→pending requests for the current session, then `/approve N` or `/deny N`
    41→directly from Telegram or WhatsApp.
    42→
    43→### Approval Policies
    44→
    45→Configure approval behavior in `moltis.toml`:
    46→
    47→```toml
    48→[tools.exec]
    49→approval_mode = "always"  # always require approval
    50→# approval_mode = "smart" # auto-approve safe commands (default)
    51→# approval_mode = "never" # dangerous: never require approval
    52→```
    53→
    54→**Recommendation**: Keep `approval_mode = "smart"` (the default) for most use
    55→cases. Only use `"never"` in fully automated, sandboxed environments.
    56→
    57→### Built-in Dangerous Command Blocklist
    58→
    59→Even with `approval_mode = "never"` or `security_level = "full"`, Moltis
    60→maintains a safety floor: a hardcoded set of regex patterns for the most
    61→critical destructive commands (e.g. `rm -rf /`, `git reset --hard`,
    62→`DROP TABLE`, `mkfs`, `terraform destroy`). Matching commands always require
    63→approval regardless of configuration.
    64→
    65→Users can override specific patterns by adding matching entries to their
    66→`allowlist` in `moltis.toml`. The blocklist only applies to host execution;
    67→sandboxed commands are already isolated.
    68→
    69→### Destructive Command Guard (dcg)
    70→
    71→For broader coverage beyond the built-in blocklist, install the
    72→[Destructive Command Guard](https://github.com/Dicklesworthstone/destructive_command_guard)
    73→(dcg) as a hook. dcg adds 49+ pattern categories including heredoc/inline-script
    74→scanning, database, cloud, and infrastructure patterns.
    75→
    76→See [Hooks: Destructive Command Guard](hooks.md#recommended-destructive-command-guard-dcg)
    77→for setup instructions.
    78→
    79→dcg complements (does not replace) sandbox isolation and the approval system.
    80→
    81→## Sandbox Isolation
    82→
    83→Commands execute inside isolated containers (Docker or Apple Container) by
    84→default. This protects your host system from:
    85→
    86→- Accidental file deletion or modification
    87→- Malicious code execution
    88→- Resource exhaustion (memory, CPU, disk)
    89→
    90→See [sandbox.md](sandbox.md) for backend configuration.
    91→
    92→### Resource Limits
    93→
    94→```toml
    95→[tools.exec.sandbox.resource_limits]
    96→memory_limit = "512M"
    97→cpu_quota = 1.0
    98→pids_max = 256
    99→```
   100→
   101→### Network Isolation
   102→
   103→Sandbox containers have no network access by default (`no_network = true`).
   104→
   105→For tasks that need internet access, [trusted network mode](trusted-network.md)
   106→provides a proxy-filtered allowlist — only connections to explicitly approved
   107→domains are permitted. All requests (allowed and denied) are recorded in the
   108→network audit log for review.
   109→
   110→## Channel Authorization
   111→
   112→Channels (Telegram, Slack, etc.) allow external parties to interact with your
   113→Moltis agent. This requires careful access control.
   114→
   115→### Sender Allowlisting
   116→
   117→When a new sender contacts the agent through a channel, they are placed in a
   118→pending queue. You must explicitly approve or deny each sender before they can
   119→interact with the agent.
   120→
   121→```
   122→UI: Settings > Channels > Pending Senders
   123→```
   124→
   125→### Per-Channel Permissions
   126→
   127→Each channel can have different permission levels:
   128→
   129→- **Read-only**: Sender can ask questions, agent responds
   130→- **Execute**: Sender can trigger actions (with approval still required)
   131→- **Admin**: Full access including configuration changes
   132→
   133→### Channel Isolation
   134→
   135→Channels run in isolated sessions by default. A malicious message from one
   136→channel cannot affect another channel's session or the main UI session.
   137→
   138→## Cron Job Security
   139→
   140→Scheduled tasks (cron jobs) can run agent turns automatically. Security
   141→considerations:
   142→
   143→### Rate Limiting
   144→
   145→To prevent prompt injection attacks from rapidly creating many cron jobs:
   146→
   147→```toml
   148→[cron]
   149→rate_limit_max = 10           # max jobs per window
   150→rate_limit_window_secs = 60   # window duration (1 minute)
   151→```
   152→
   153→This limits job creation to 10 per minute by default. System jobs (like
   154→heartbeat) bypass this limit.
   155→
   156→### Job Notifications
   157→
   158→When cron jobs are created, updated, or removed, Moltis broadcasts events:
   159→
   160→- `cron.job.created` - A new job was created
   161→- `cron.job.updated` - An existing job was modified
   162→- `cron.job.removed` - A job was deleted
   163→
   164→Monitor these events to detect suspicious automated job creation.
   165→
   166→### Sandbox for Cron Jobs
   167→
   168→Cron job execution uses sandbox isolation by default:
   169→
   170→```toml
   171→# Per-job configuration
   172→[cron.job.sandbox]
   173→enabled = true              # run in sandbox (default)
   174→# image = "custom:latest"   # optional custom image
   175→```
   176→
   177→## Identity Protection
   178→
   179→The agent's identity fields (name, emoji, creature, vibe) are stored in `IDENTITY.md`
   180→YAML frontmatter at the workspace root (`data_dir`).
   181→User profile fields are stored in `USER.md` YAML frontmatter at the same location.
   182→The personality text is stored separately in `SOUL.md` at the workspace root (`data_dir`).
   183→Tool guidance is stored in `TOOLS.md` at the workspace root (`data_dir`) and is injected
   184→as workspace context in the system prompt.
   185→Modifying identity requires the `operator.write` scope, not just `operator.read`.
   186→
   187→This prevents prompt injection attacks from subtly modifying the agent's
   188→personality to make it more compliant with malicious requests.
   189→
   190→## API Authorization
   191→
   192→The gateway API uses role-based access control with scopes:
   193→
   194→| Scope | Permissions |
   195→|-------|-------------|
   196→| `operator.read` | View status, list jobs, read history |
   197→| `operator.write` | Send messages, create jobs, modify configuration |
   198→| `operator.admin` | All permissions (includes all other scopes) |
   199→| `operator.approvals` | Handle command approval requests |
   200→| `operator.pairing` | Manage device/node pairing |
   201→
   202→### API Keys
   203→
   204→API keys authenticate external tools and scripts connecting to Moltis. Keys
   205→**must specify at least one scope** — keys without scopes are denied access
   206→(least-privilege by default).
   207→
   208→#### Creating API Keys
   209→
   210→**Web UI**: Settings > Security > API Keys
   211→
   212→1. Enter a label describing the key's purpose
   213→2. Select the required scopes
   214→3. Click "Generate key"
   215→4. **Copy the key immediately** — it's only shown once
   216→
   217→**CLI**:
   218→
   219→```bash
   220→# Scoped key (comma-separated scopes)
   221→moltis auth create-api-key --label "Monitor" --scopes "operator.read"
   222→moltis auth create-api-key --label "Automation" --scopes "operator.read,operator.write"
   223→moltis auth create-api-key --label "CI pipeline" --scopes "operator.admin"
   224→```
   225→
   226→#### Using API Keys
   227→
   228→Pass the key in the `connect` handshake over WebSocket:
   229→
   230→```json
   231→{
   232→  "method": "connect",
   233→  "params": {
   234→    "client": { "id": "my-tool", "version": "1.0.0" },
   235→    "auth": { "api_key": "mk_abc123..." }
   236→  }
   237→}
   238→```
   239→
   240→Or use Bearer authentication for REST API calls:
   241→
   242→```
   243→Authorization: Bearer mk_abc123...
   244→```
   245→
   246→#### Scope Recommendations
   247→
   248→| Use Case | Recommended Scopes |
   249→|----------|-------------------|
   250→| Read-only monitoring | `operator.read` |
   251→| Automated workflows | `operator.read`, `operator.write` |
   252→| Approval handling | `operator.read`, `operator.approvals` |
   253→| Full automation | `operator.admin` |
   254→
   255→**Best practice**: Use the minimum necessary scopes. If a key only needs to
   256→read status and logs, don't grant `operator.write`.
   257→
   258→#### Backward Compatibility
   259→
   260→Existing API keys created without scopes will be **denied access** until
   261→scopes are added. Re-create keys with explicit scopes to restore access.
   262→
   263→## Encryption at Rest
   264→
   265→Sensitive data in the SQLite database (environment variables containing
   266→API keys, tokens, etc.) is encrypted at rest using XChaCha20-Poly1305.
   267→The encryption key is derived from the user's password via Argon2id.
   268→
   269→The vault initializes when a first password is set (during setup or later
   270→in Settings > Authentication), unseals automatically on login, and
   271→re-seals on server restart. A recovery key is provided at initialization
   272→for emergency access.
   273→
   274→When the vault is sealed, a middleware layer blocks vault-protected API
   275→requests with `423 Locked`. Session history and bootstrap endpoints remain
   276→available because those payloads are not yet encrypted at rest.
   277→
   278→For full details on the key hierarchy, vault states, API endpoints, and
   279→cryptographic parameters, see [Encryption at Rest (Vault)](vault.md).
   280→
   281→## Network Security
   282→
   283→### TLS Encryption
   284→
   285→HTTPS is enabled by default with auto-generated certificates:
   286→
   287→```toml
   288→[tls]
   289→enabled = true
   290→auto_generate = true
   291→```
   292→
   293→For production, use certificates from a trusted CA or configure custom
   294→certificates.
   295→
   296→### Origin Validation
   297→
   298→WebSocket connections validate the `Origin` header to prevent cross-site
   299→WebSocket hijacking (CSWSH). Connections from untrusted origins are rejected.
   300→
   301→### SSRF Protection
   302→
   303→The `web_fetch` tool resolves DNS and blocks requests to private IP ranges
   304→(loopback, RFC 1918, link-local, CGNAT). This prevents server-side request
   305→forgery attacks.
   306→
   307→To allow access to trusted private networks (e.g. Docker sibling containers),
   308→add their CIDR ranges to `ssrf_allowlist`:
   309→
   310→```toml
   311→[tools.web.fetch]
   312→ssrf_allowlist = ["172.22.0.0/16"]
   313→```
   314→
   315→**Warning:** Only add networks you trust. The allowlist bypasses SSRF protection
   316→for the listed ranges. Never add cloud metadata ranges (`169.254.169.254/32`)
   317→unless you understand the risk.
   318→
   319→## Authentication
   320→
   321→Moltis uses a unified auth gate that applies a single `check_auth()`
   322→function to every request. This prevents split-brain bugs where different
   323→code paths disagree on auth status.
   324→
   325→For full details — including the decision matrix, credential types, API
   326→key scopes, session management endpoints, and WebSocket auth — see the
   327→dedicated [Authentication](authentication.md) page.
   328→
   329→### Three-Tier Model (summary)
   330→
   331→| Tier | Condition | Behaviour |
   332→|------|-----------|-----------|
   333→| **1** | Password/passkey is configured | Auth **always** required (any IP) |
   334→| **2** | No credentials + direct local connection | Full access (dev convenience) |
   335→| **3** | No credentials + remote/proxied connection | Onboarding only (setup code required) |
   336→
   337→## HTTP Endpoint Throttling
   338→
   339→Moltis includes built-in per-IP endpoint throttling to reduce brute force
   340→attempts and traffic spikes, but only when auth is required for the current
   341→request.
   342→
   343→Throttling is bypassed when a request is already authenticated, when auth is
   344→explicitly disabled, or when setup is incomplete and local Tier-2 access is
   345→allowed.
   346→
   347→### Default Limits
   348→
   349→| Scope | Default |
   350→|------|---------|
   351→| `POST /api/auth/login` | 5 requests per 60 seconds |
   352→| Other `/api/auth/*` | 120 requests per 60 seconds |
   353→| Other `/api/*` | 180 requests per 60 seconds |
   354→| `/ws/chat` upgrade | 30 requests per 60 seconds |
   355→| `/ws` upgrade | 30 requests per 60 seconds |
   356→
   357→### When Limits Are Hit
   358→
   359→- API endpoints return `429 Too Many Requests`
   360→- Responses include `Retry-After`
   361→- JSON responses include `retry_after_seconds`
   362→
   363→### Reverse Proxy Behavior
   364→
   365→When `MOLTIS_BEHIND_PROXY=true`, throttling is keyed by forwarded client IP
   366→headers (`X-Forwarded-For`, `X-Real-IP`, `CF-Connecting-IP`) instead of the
   367→direct socket address.
   368→
   369→### Production Guidance
   370→
   371→Built-in throttling is the first layer. For internet-facing deployments, add
   372→edge rate limits at your reverse proxy or WAF as a second layer (IP reputation,
   373→burst controls, geo rules, bot filtering).
   374→
   375→## Reverse Proxy Deployments
   376→
   377→Running Moltis behind a reverse proxy (Caddy, nginx, Traefik, etc.)
   378→requires understanding how authentication interacts with loopback
   379→connections.
   380→
   381→### The problem
   382→
   383→When Moltis binds to `127.0.0.1` and a proxy on the same machine
   384→forwards traffic to it, **every** incoming TCP connection appears to
   385→originate from `127.0.0.1` — including requests from the public
   386→internet.  A naive "trust all loopback connections" check would bypass
   387→authentication for all proxied traffic.
   388→
   389→This is the same class of vulnerability as
   390→[CVE-2026-25253](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/security/advisories/GHSA-g8p2-7wf7-98mq),
   391→which allowed one-click remote code execution on OpenClaw through
   392→authentication token exfiltration and cross-site WebSocket hijacking.
   393→
   394→### How Moltis handles it
   395→
   396→Moltis uses the per-request `is_local_connection()` check described
   397→above.  Most reverse proxies add forwarding headers or change the
   398→`Host` header, which automatically triggers the "remote" classification.
   399→
   400→For proxies that **strip all signals** (e.g. a bare nginx `proxy_pass`
   401→that rewrites `Host` to the upstream address and adds no `X-Forwarded-For`),
   402→use the `MOLTIS_BEHIND_PROXY` environment variable as a hard override:
   403→
   404→```bash
   405→MOLTIS_BEHIND_PROXY=true moltis
   406→```
   407→
   408→When this variable is set, **all** connections are treated as remote —
   409→no loopback bypass, no exceptions.
   410→
   411→### Deploying behind a proxy
   412→
   413→1. **Set `MOLTIS_BEHIND_PROXY=true`** if your proxy does not add
   414→   forwarding headers (safest option — eliminates any ambiguity).
   415→
   416→2. **Set a password or register a passkey** during initial setup.
   417→   Once a password is configured (Tier 1), authentication is required
   418→   for all traffic regardless of `is_local_connection()`.
   419→
   420→3. **WebSocket proxying** must preserve browser origin host info
   421→   (`Host`, or `X-Forwarded-Host` if `Host` is rewritten). Moltis
   422→   validates same-origin on WebSocket upgrades to prevent cross-site
   423→   WebSocket hijacking (CSWSH).
   424→
   425→4. **TLS termination** should happen at the proxy. Run Moltis with
   426→   `--no-tls` (or `MOLTIS_NO_TLS=true`) in this mode.
   427→
   428→   If your browser is being redirected to `https://<domain>:13131`,
   429→   Moltis TLS is still enabled while your proxy upstream is plain HTTP.
   430→
   431→5. **Advanced TLS upstream mode** (optional): if your proxy connects to
   432→   Moltis using HTTPS upstream (or TCP TLS passthrough), you may keep
   433→   Moltis TLS enabled. Set `MOLTIS_ALLOW_TLS_BEHIND_PROXY=true` to
   434→   acknowledge this non-default setup.
   435→
   436→### Nginx (direct config example)
   437→
   438→If HTTP works but WebSockets fail, make sure your location block includes
   439→`proxy_http_version 1.1;` and upgrade headers.
   440→
   441→```nginx
   442→location / {
   443→    proxy_pass http://172.17.0.1:13131;
   444→    proxy_set_header Host $host;
   445→    proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Host $host;
   446→    proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
   447→    proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
   448→    proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
   449→
   450→    # WebSocket upgrade support
   451→    proxy_http_version 1.1;
   452→    proxy_set_header Upgrade $http_upgrade;
   453→    proxy_set_header Connection $connection_upgrade;
   454→}
   455→```
   456→
   457→If you use `$connection_upgrade`, define it once in the `http {}` block:
   458→
   459→```nginx
   460→map $http_upgrade $connection_upgrade {
   461→    default upgrade;
   462→    ''      close;
   463→}
   464→```
   465→
   466→### Nginx Proxy Manager (known-good headers)
   467→
   468→If WebSockets fail behind NPM while HTTP works, ensure:
   469→
   470→- Moltis runs with `MOLTIS_BEHIND_PROXY=true`
   471→- For standard edge TLS termination, Moltis runs with `--no-tls`
   472→- NPM preserves browser host/origin context
   473→
   474→Use this in NPM's **Advanced** field:
   475→
   476→```nginx
   477→proxy_set_header Host $host;
   478→proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Host $host;
   479→proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
   480→proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
   481→proxy_set_header Upgrade $http_upgrade;
   482→proxy_set_header Connection "upgrade";
   483→```
   484→
   485→Upstream scheme guidance:
   486→
   487→- **Edge TLS termination (most setups)**: proxy to `http://<moltis-host>:13131`
   488→  with Moltis started using `--no-tls`
   489→- **HTTPS upstream / TLS passthrough**: proxy to `https://<moltis-host>:13131`
   490→  and set `MOLTIS_ALLOW_TLS_BEHIND_PROXY=true`
   491→
   492→### Passkeys Behind Proxies (Host Changes)
   493→
   494→WebAuthn passkeys are bound to an RP ID (domain identity), not just the
   495→server process. In practice:
   496→
   497→- If users move from one hostname to another, old passkeys for the old host
   498→  will not authenticate on the new host.
   499→- If a proxy rewrites `Host` and does not preserve browser host context,
   500→  passkey routes can fail with "no passkey config for this hostname".
   501→
   502→For stable proxy deployments, set explicit WebAuthn identity to your public
   503→domain:
   504→
   505→```bash
   506→MOLTIS_BEHIND_PROXY=true
   507→MOLTIS_NO_TLS=true
   508→MOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_RP_ID=chat.example.com
   509→MOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_ORIGIN=https://chat.example.com
   510→```
   511→
   512→Migration guidance when changing host/domain:
   513→
   514→1. Keep password login enabled during migration.
   515→2. Deploy with the new `MOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_RP_ID`/`MOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_ORIGIN`.
   516→3. Ask users to register a new passkey on the new host.
   517→4. Remove old passkeys after new-host login is confirmed.
   518→
   519→## Production Recommendations
   520→
   521→### 1. Enable Authentication
   522→
   523→By default, Moltis requires a password when accessed from non-localhost:
   524→
   525→```toml
   526→[auth]
   527→disabled = false  # keep this false in production
   528→```
   529→
   530→### 2. Use Sandbox Isolation
   531→
   532→Always run with sandbox enabled in production:
   533→
   534→```toml
   535→[tools.exec.sandbox]
   536→enabled = true
   537→backend = "auto"  # uses strongest available
   538→```
   539→
   540→### 3. Limit Rate Limits
   541→
   542→Tighten rate limits for untrusted environments:
   543→
   544→```toml
   545→[cron]
   546→rate_limit_max = 5
   547→rate_limit_window_secs = 300  # 5 per 5 minutes
   548→```
   549→
   550→### 4. Review Channel Senders
   551→
   552→Regularly audit approved senders and revoke access for unknown parties.
   553→
   554→### 5. Monitor Events
   555→
   556→Watch for these suspicious patterns:
   557→
   558→- Rapid cron job creation
   559→- Identity modification attempts
   560→- Unusual command patterns in approval requests
   561→- New channel senders from unexpected sources
   562→
   563→### 6. Network Segmentation
   564→
   565→Run Moltis on a private network or behind a reverse proxy with:
   566→
   567→- IP allowlisting
   568→- Rate limiting
   569→- Web Application Firewall (WAF) rules
   570→
   571→### 7. Keep Software Updated
   572→
   573→Subscribe to security advisories and update promptly when vulnerabilities are
   574→disclosed.
   575→
   576→## Release Signing and Verification
   577→
   578→All release artifacts are signed with three independent methods:
   579→
   580→1. **[GitHub artifact attestations](https://github.com/moltis-org/moltis/attestations)**
   581→   (automated in CI) — cryptographic provenance records tied to the repository,
   582→   workflow, and commit SHA; provides SLSA v1.0 Build Level 2 guarantees;
   583→   verifiable with `gh attestation verify`
   584→2. **Sigstore keyless signing** (automated in CI) — proves the artifact was
   585→   built by the `moltis-org/moltis` GitHub Actions pipeline; recorded in
   586→   Sigstore's Rekor transparency log
   587→3. **GPG signing** (maintainer's YubiKey hardware key) — proves a specific
   588→   maintainer authorized the release
   589→
   590→Checksums (SHA-256 and SHA-512) are generated for every artifact.
   591→
   592→See [Release Verification](release-verification.md) for detailed verification
   593→instructions, artifact file extensions, and maintainer signing workflow.
   594→
   595→## Reporting Security Issues
   596→
   597→Report security vulnerabilities privately to the maintainers. Do not open
   598→public issues for security bugs.
   599→
   600→See the repository's SECURITY.md for contact information.
   601→
```

   111→#[async_trait]
   112→impl moltis_tools::exec::EnvVarProvider for CredentialEnvVarProvider {
   113→    async fn get_env_vars(&self) -> Vec<(String, Secret<String>)> {
   114→        match self.store.get_all_env_values().await {
   115→            Ok(values) => values
   116→                .into_iter()
   117→                .map(|(key, value)| (key, Secret::new(value)))
   118→                .collect(),
   119→            Err(error) => {
   120→                warn!(error = %error, "failed to load runtime env overrides for tools");
   121→                Vec::new()
   122→            },
   123→        }
   124→    }
   125→}
   126→
   127→async fn build_webauthn_registry(
   128→    config: &moltis_config::MoltisConfig,
   129→    port: u16,
   130→) -> anyhow::Result<Option<crate::auth_webauthn::SharedWebAuthnRegistry>> {
   131→    let default_scheme = if config.tls.enabled {
   132→        "https"
   133→    } else {
   134→        "http"
   135→    };
   136→
   137→    let explicit_rp_id = std::env::var("MOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_RP_ID")
   138→        .or_else(|_| std::env::var("APP_DOMAIN"))
   139→        .or_else(|_| std::env::var("RENDER_EXTERNAL_HOSTNAME"))
   140→        .or_else(|_| std::env::var("FLY_APP_NAME").map(|name| format!("{name}.fly.dev")))
   141→        .or_else(|_| std::env::var("RAILWAY_PUBLIC_DOMAIN"))
   142→        .ok();
   143→    let explicit_origin = std::env::var("MOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_ORIGIN")
   144→        .or_else(|_| std::env::var("APP_URL"))
   145→        .or_else(|_| std::env::var("RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL"))
   146→        .ok();
   147→
   148→    let mut wa_registry = crate::auth_webauthn::WebAuthnRegistry::new();
   149→    let mut any_ok = false;
   150→
   151→    let mut try_add = |rp_id: &str, origin_str: &str, extras: &[webauthn_rs::prelude::Url]| {
   152→        let rp_id = crate::auth_webauthn::normalize_host(rp_id);
   153→        if rp_id.is_empty() || wa_registry.contains_host(&rp_id) {
   154→            return;
   155→        }
   156→        let Ok(origin_url) = webauthn_rs::prelude::Url::parse(origin_str) else {
   157→            tracing::warn!("invalid WebAuthn origin URL '{origin_str}'");
   158→            return;
   159→        };
   160→        match crate::auth_webauthn::WebAuthnState::new(&rp_id, &origin_url, extras) {
   161→            Ok(wa) => {
   162→                info!(rp_id = %rp_id, origins = ?wa.get_allowed_origins(), "WebAuthn RP registered");
   163→                wa_registry.add(rp_id.clone(), wa);
   164→                any_ok = true;
   165→            },
   166→            Err(e) => tracing::warn!(rp_id = %rp_id, "failed to init WebAuthn: {e}"),
   167→        }
   168→    };
   169→
   170→    if let Some(ref rp_id) = explicit_rp_id {
   171→        let origin = explicit_origin
   172→            .clone()
   173→            .unwrap_or_else(|| format!("https://{rp_id}"));
   174→        try_add(rp_id, &origin, &[]);
   175→    } else {
   176→        let localhost_origin = format!("{default_scheme}://localhost:{port}");
   177→        let moltis_localhost: Vec<webauthn_rs::prelude::Url> = webauthn_rs::prelude::Url::parse(
   178→            &format!("{default_scheme}://moltis.localhost:{port}"),
   179→        )
   180→        .into_iter()
   181→        .collect();
   182→        try_add("localhost", &localhost_origin, &moltis_localhost);
   183→
   184→        let instance_slug_value = instance_slug(config);
   185→        if instance_slug_value != "localhost" {
   186→            let bot_origin = format!("{default_scheme}://{instance_slug_value}:{port}");
   187→            try_add(&instance_slug_value, &bot_origin, &[]);
   188→
   189→            let bot_local = format!("{instance_slug_value}.local");
   190→            let bot_local_origin = format!("{default_scheme}://{bot_local}:{port}");
   191→            try_add(&bot_local, &bot_local_origin, &[]);
   192→        }
   193→
   194→        if let Ok(hn) = hostname::get() {
   195→            let hn_str = hn.to_string_lossy();
   196→            if hn_str != "localhost" {
   197→                let local_name = if hn_str.ends_with(".local") {
   198→                    hn_str.to_string()
   199→                } else {
   200→                    format!("{hn_str}.local")
   201→                };
   202→                let local_origin = format!("{default_scheme}://{local_name}:{port}");
   203→                try_add(&local_name, &local_origin, &[]);
   204→
   205→                let bare = hn_str.strip_suffix(".local").unwrap_or(&hn_str);
   206→                if bare != local_name {
   207→                    let bare_origin = format!("{default_scheme}://{bare}:{port}");
   208→                    try_add(bare, &bare_origin, &[]);
   209→                }
   210→            }
   211→        }
   212→    }
   213→
   214→    if any_ok {
   215→        info!(origins = ?wa_registry.get_all_origins(), "WebAuthn passkeys enabled");
   216→        Ok(Some(Arc::new(tokio::sync::RwLock::new(wa_registry))))
   217→    } else {
   218→        Ok(None)
   219→    }
   220→}

   287→    #[cfg(feature = "metrics")]

   374→            #[cfg(feature = "vault")]

   381→            #[cfg(not(feature = "vault"))]
   382→            {
   383→                inner

   386→        let _ = state.webhook_store.set(Arc::clone(&webhook_store));
   387→        let _ = state.webhook_worker_tx.set(webhook_tx);
   388→
   389→        let worker_store = Arc::clone(&webhook_store);
   390→        let worker_state_ref = Arc::clone(&state);
   391→        let worker = moltis_webhooks::worker::WebhookWorker::new(
   392→            webhook_rx,
   393→            worker_store,
   394→            Arc::new(move |req: moltis_webhooks::worker::ExecuteRequest| {
   395→                let chat_state = Arc::clone(&worker_state_ref);
   396→                Box::pin(async move {
   397→                    let chat = chat_state.chat().await;
   398→                    let mut params = serde_json::json!({
   399→                        "text": req.message,
   400→                        "_session_key": req.session_key,
   401→                    });
   402→                    if let Some(ref model) = req.model {
   403→                        params["model"] = serde_json::Value::String(model.clone());
   404→                    }
   405→                    if let Some(ref agent_id) = req.agent_id {
   406→                        params["agent_id"] = serde_json::Value::String(agent_id.clone());
   407→                    }
   408→                    if let Some(ref tool_policy) = req.tool_policy {
   409→                        params["_tool_policy"] = serde_json::to_value(tool_policy)
   410→                            .map_err(|error| anyhow::anyhow!(error))?;
   411→                    }
   412→                    let result = chat
   413→                        .send_sync(params)
   414→                        .await
   415→                        .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("{e}"))?;
   416→                    let input_tokens = result.get("inputTokens").and_then(|v| v.as_i64());
   417→                    let output_tokens = result.get("outputTokens").and_then(|v| v.as_i64());
   418→                    let output = result
   419→                        .get("text")
   420→                        .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
   421→                        .map(String::from);
   422→                    Ok(moltis_webhooks::worker::ProcessResult {
   423→                        output,
   424→                        input_tokens,
   425→                        output_tokens,
   426→                        session_key: req.session_key,
   427→                    })
   428→                })
   429→            }),
   430→        );
   431→        tokio::spawn(worker.run());
   432→    }
   433→
   434→    let _ = deferred_state.set(Arc::clone(&state));
   435→
   436→    #[cfg(feature = "local-llm")]
   437→    if let Some(svc) = &local_llm_service {

   440→

   442→        state: Arc::clone(&state),

   444→    live_model_service.set_state(crate::chat::GatewayChatRuntime::from_state(Arc::clone(
   445→        &state,

   447→

   452→
   453→    {
   454→        let mut inner = state.inner.write().await;
   455→        inner.discovered_hooks = discovered_hooks_info;
   456→        inner.disabled_hooks = persisted_disabled;
   457→        inner.shiki_cdn_url = config.server.shiki_cdn_url.clone();
   458→        #[cfg(feature = "metrics")]
   459→        {
   460→            inner.metrics_history =
   461→                crate::state::MetricsHistory::new(config.metrics.history_points);
   462→        }
   463→    }
   464→
   465→    let setup_code_display =
   466→        if !credential_store.is_setup_complete() && !credential_store.is_auth_disabled() {
   467→            let code = std::env::var("MOLTIS_E2E_SETUP_CODE")
   468→                .unwrap_or_else(|_| auth::generate_setup_code());
   469→            {
   470→                let mut inner = state.inner.write().await;
   471→                inner.setup_code = Some(Secret::new(code.clone()));
   472→                inner.setup_code_created_at = Some(std::time::Instant::now());
   473→            }
   474→            Some(code)
   475→        } else {
   476→            None
   477→        };
   478→
   479→    #[cfg(feature = "tailscale")]
   480→    let tailscale_mode: TailscaleMode = {
   481→        let mode_str = tailscale_mode_override.unwrap_or_else(|| config.tailscale.mode.clone());
   482→        mode_str.parse().unwrap_or(TailscaleMode::Off)
   483→    };
   484→    #[cfg(feature = "tailscale")]
   485→    let tailscale_reset_on_exit =
   486→        tailscale_reset_on_exit_override.unwrap_or(config.tailscale.reset_on_exit);
   487→
   488→    #[cfg(feature = "tailscale")]
   489→    if tailscale_mode != TailscaleMode::Off {
   490→        validate_tailscale_config(tailscale_mode, &bind, credential_store.is_setup_complete())?;
   491→    }
   492→
   493→    let webauthn_registry = build_webauthn_registry(&config, port).await?;
   494→
   495→    if startup_discovery_pending.is_empty() {
   496→        debug!("startup model discovery skipped, no pending provider discoveries");
   497→    } else {
   498→        let registry_for_startup_discovery = Arc::clone(&registry);
   499→        let state_for_startup_discovery = Arc::clone(&state);
   500→        let provider_config_for_startup_discovery = effective_providers.clone();
   501→        let provider_config_for_registry_rebuild = provider_config_for_startup_discovery.clone();
   502→        let global_cw_overrides = moltis_providers::extract_cw_overrides(&config.models);
   503→        let env_overrides_for_startup_discovery = config_env_overrides.clone();
   504→        tokio::spawn(async move {
   505→            let startup_discovery_started = std::time::Instant::now();
   506→            let prefetched = match tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || {
   507→                ProviderRegistry::collect_discoveries(startup_discovery_pending)
   508→            })
   509→            .await
   510→            {
   511→                Ok(prefetched) => prefetched,
   512→                Err(error) => {
   513→                    warn!(
   514→                        error = %error,
   515→                        "startup background model discovery worker failed while collecting results"
   516→                    );
   517→                    return;
   518→                },
   519→            };
   520→
   521→            let prefetched_models: usize = prefetched.values().map(Vec::len).sum();
   522→            let mut new_registry = match tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || {
   523→                ProviderRegistry::from_config_with_prefetched(
   524→                    &provider_config_for_registry_rebuild,
   525→                    &env_overrides_for_startup_discovery,
   526→                    &prefetched,
   527→                    global_cw_overrides.clone(),
   528→                )
   529→            })
   530→            .await
   531→            {
   532→                Ok(new_registry) => new_registry,
   533→                Err(error) => {
   534→                    warn!(
   535→                        error = %error,
   536→                        "startup background model discovery worker failed while rebuilding registry"
   537→                    );
   538→                    return;
   539→                },
   540→            };
   541→
   542→            restore_saved_local_llm_models(
   543→                &mut new_registry,
   544→                &provider_config_for_startup_discovery,
   545→            );
   546→            let provider_summary = new_registry.provider_summary();
   547→            let model_count = new_registry.list_models().len();
   548→            {
   549→                let mut reg = registry_for_startup_discovery.write().await;
   550→                *reg = new_registry;
   551→            }
   552→
   553→            info!(
   554→                provider_summary = %provider_summary,
   555→                models = model_count,
   556→                prefetched_models,
   557→                elapsed_ms = startup_discovery_started.elapsed().as_millis(),
   558→                "startup background model discovery complete, provider registry updated"
   559→            );
   560→
   561→            broadcast(
   562→                &state_for_startup_discovery,
   563→                "models.updated",
   564→                serde_json::json!({
   565→                    "reason": "startup-discovery",
   566→                    "models": model_count,
   567→                    "providerSummary": provider_summary,
   568→                }),
   569→                BroadcastOpts::default(),
   570→            )
   571→            .await;
   572→        });
   573→    }
   574→
   575→    {
   576→        let mut inner = state.inner.write().await;
   577→        inner.heartbeat_config = config.heartbeat.clone();
   578→        inner.channels_offered = config.channels.offered.clone();
   579→    }
   580→    #[cfg(feature = "graphql")]
   581→    state.set_graphql_enabled(config.graphql.enabled);
   582→
   583→    {
   584→        let broadcaster: Arc<dyn moltis_tools::exec::ApprovalBroadcaster> =
   585→            Arc::new(GatewayApprovalBroadcaster::new(Arc::clone(&state)));
   586→        let env_provider: Arc<dyn moltis_tools::exec::EnvVarProvider> =
   587→            Arc::new(CredentialEnvVarProvider {
   588→                store: Arc::clone(&credential_store),
   589→            });
   590→        let eq = cron_service.events_queue().clone();
   591→        let cs = Arc::clone(&cron_service);
   592→        let exec_cb: moltis_tools::exec::ExecCompletionFn = Arc::new(move |event| {
   593→            let summary = format!("Command `{}` exited {}", event.command, event.exit_code);
   594→            let eq = Arc::clone(&eq);
   595→            let cs = Arc::clone(&cs);
   596→            tokio::spawn(async move {
   597→                eq.enqueue(summary, "exec-event".into()).await;
   598→                cs.wake("exec-event").await;
   599→            });
   600→        });
   601→        let mut exec_tool = moltis_tools::exec::ExecTool::default()
   602→            .with_default_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(
   603→                config.tools.exec.default_timeout_secs,
   604→            ))
   605→            .with_max_output_bytes(config.tools.exec.max_output_bytes)
   606→            .with_approval(Arc::clone(&approval_manager), Arc::clone(&broadcaster))
   607→            .with_sandbox_router(Arc::clone(&sandbox_router))
   608→            .with_env_provider(Arc::clone(&env_provider))
   609→            .with_completion_callback(exec_cb);
   610→
   611→        {
   612→            let provider = Arc::new(crate::node_exec::GatewayNodeExecProvider::new(
   613→                Arc::clone(&state),
   614→                Arc::clone(&state.node_count),
   615→                Arc::clone(&state.ssh_target_count),
   616→                config.tools.exec.ssh_target.clone(),
   617→                config.tools.exec.max_output_bytes,
   618→            ));
   619→            let default_node = match config.tools.exec.host.as_str() {
   620→                "node" => config.tools.exec.node.clone(),
   621→                "ssh" => config.tools.exec.ssh_target.clone(),
   622→                _ => None,
   623→            };
   624→            exec_tool = exec_tool.with_node_provider(provider, default_node);
   625→        }
   626→
   627→        let cron_tool = moltis_tools::cron_tool::CronTool::new(Arc::clone(&cron_service));
   628→
   629→        let mut tool_registry = moltis_agents::tool_registry::ToolRegistry::new();
   630→        let process_tool = moltis_tools::process::ProcessTool::new()
   631→            .with_sandbox_router(Arc::clone(&sandbox_router));
   632→
   633→        let sandbox_packages_tool = moltis_tools::sandbox_packages::SandboxPackagesTool::new()
   634→            .with_sandbox_router(Arc::clone(&sandbox_router));
   635→
   636→        tool_registry.register(Box::new(exec_tool));
   637→        tool_registry.register(Box::new(moltis_tools::calc::CalcTool::new()));
   638→        #[cfg(feature = "fs-tools")]
   639→        {
   640→            use moltis_config::schema::FsBinaryPolicy;
   641→            let fs_cfg = &config.tools.fs;
   642→            let path_policy = match moltis_tools::fs::FsPathPolicy::new(
   643→                &fs_cfg.allow_paths,
   644→                &fs_cfg.deny_paths,
   645→            ) {
   646→                Ok(p) => {
   647→                    if p.is_empty() {
   648→                        None
   649→                    } else {
   650→                        Some(p)
   651→                    }
   652→                },
   653→                Err(e) => {
   654→                    warn!(error = %e, "invalid tools.fs path policy — fs tools will run without path allow/deny");
   655→                    None
   656→                },
   657→            };
   658→            let workspace_root = fs_cfg.workspace_root.as_ref().map(PathBuf::from);
   659→            let binary_policy = match fs_cfg.binary_policy {
   660→                FsBinaryPolicy::Reject => moltis_tools::fs::BinaryPolicy::Reject,
   661→                FsBinaryPolicy::Base64 => moltis_tools::fs::BinaryPolicy::Base64,
   662→            };
   663→            let checkpoint_manager = if fs_cfg.checkpoint_before_mutation {
   664→                Some(Arc::new(moltis_tools::checkpoints::CheckpointManager::new(
   665→                    moltis_config::data_dir(),
   666→                )))
   667→            } else {
   668→                None
   669→            };
   670→            let shared_fs_state = if fs_cfg.track_reads {
   671→                Some(moltis_tools::fs::new_fs_state(
   672→                    fs_cfg.must_read_before_write,
   673→                ))
   674→            } else {
   675→                None
   676→            };
   677→            let ctx = moltis_tools::fs::FsToolsContext {
   678→                workspace_root,
   679→                fs_state: shared_fs_state.clone(),
   680→                path_policy,
   681→                binary_policy,
   682→                respect_gitignore: fs_cfg.respect_gitignore,
   683→                checkpoint_manager,
   684→                sandbox_router: Some(Arc::clone(&sandbox_router)),
   685→                approval_manager: fs_cfg
   686→                    .require_approval
   687→                    .then(|| Arc::clone(&approval_manager)),
   688→                broadcaster: fs_cfg.require_approval.then(|| Arc::clone(&broadcaster)),
   689→                max_read_bytes: Some(fs_cfg.max_read_bytes),
   690→                context_window_tokens: fs_cfg.context_window_tokens,
   691→            };
   692→            moltis_tools::fs::register_fs_tools(&mut tool_registry, ctx);
   693→            if let Some(message) = fs_tools_host_warning_message(&sandbox_router) {
   694→                warn!("{message}");
   695→            }
   696→        }
   697→        #[cfg(feature = "wasm")]
   698→        {
   699→            let wasm_limits = sandbox_router
   700→                .config()
   701→                .wasm_tool_limits
   702→                .clone()
   703→                .unwrap_or_default();
   704→            let epoch_interval_ms = sandbox_router
   705→                .config()
   706→                .wasm_epoch_interval_ms
   707→                .unwrap_or(100);
   708→            let brave_api_key = config
   709→                .tools
   710→                .web
   711→                .search
   712→                .api_key
   713→                .as_ref()
   714→                .map(|s| s.expose_secret().clone())
   715→                .or_else(|| env_value_with_overrides(&runtime_env_overrides, "BRAVE_API_KEY"))
   716→                .filter(|k| !k.trim().is_empty());
   717→            if let Err(e) = moltis_tools::wasm_tool_runner::register_wasm_tools(
   718→                &mut tool_registry,
   719→                &wasm_limits,
   720→                epoch_interval_ms,
   721→                config.tools.web.fetch.timeout_seconds,
   722→                config.tools.web.fetch.cache_ttl_minutes,
   723→                config.tools.web.search.timeout_seconds,
   724→                config.tools.web.search.cache_ttl_minutes,
   725→                brave_api_key.as_deref(),
   726→            ) {
   727→                warn!(%e, "wasm tool registration failed");
   728→            }
   729→        }
   730→        tool_registry.register(Box::new(process_tool));
   731→        tool_registry.register(Box::new(sandbox_packages_tool));
   732→        tool_registry.register(Box::new(cron_tool));
   733→        tool_registry.register(Box::new(crate::channel_agent_tools::SendMessageTool::new(
   734→            Arc::clone(&state.services.channel),
   735→        )));
   736→        {
   737→            let tp = Arc::clone(&msteams_webhook_plugin);
   738→            tool_registry.register(Box::new(
   739→                crate::teams_agent_tools::TeamsSearchMessagesTool::new(Arc::clone(&tp)),
   740→            ));
   741→            tool_registry.register(Box::new(
   742→                crate::teams_agent_tools::TeamsMemberInfoTool::new(Arc::clone(&tp)),
   743→            ));
   744→            tool_registry.register(Box::new(
   745→                crate::teams_agent_tools::TeamsPinMessageTool::new(Arc::clone(&tp)),
   746→            ));
   747→            tool_registry.register(Box::new(
   748→                crate::teams_agent_tools::TeamsEditMessageTool::new(Arc::clone(&tp)),
   749→            ));
   750→            tool_registry.register(Box::new(
   751→                crate::teams_agent_tools::TeamsReadMessageTool::new(Arc::clone(&tp)),
   752→            ));
   753→        }
   754→        tool_registry.register(Box::new(
   755→            crate::channel_agent_tools::UpdateChannelSettingsTool::new(
   756→                Arc::clone(&state.services.channel),
   757→                state.services.channel_store.clone(),
   758→            ),
   759→        ));
   760→        tool_registry.register(Box::new(
   761→            moltis_tools::send_image::SendImageTool::new()
   762→                .with_sandbox_router(Arc::clone(&sandbox_router)),
   763→        ));
   764→        tool_registry.register(Box::new(
   765→            moltis_tools::send_document::SendDocumentTool::new()
   766→                .with_sandbox_router(Arc::clone(&sandbox_router))
   767→                .with_session_store(Arc::clone(&session_store)),
   768→        ));
   769→        if let Some(t) = moltis_tools::web_search::WebSearchTool::from_config_with_env_overrides(
   770→            &config.tools.web.search,
   771→            &runtime_env_overrides,
   772→        ) {
   773→            #[cfg(feature = "firecrawl")]
   774→            let t = t.with_firecrawl_config(&config.tools.web.firecrawl);
   775→            tool_registry.register(Box::new(t.with_env_provider(Arc::clone(&env_provider))));
   776→        }
   777→        if let Some(t) = moltis_tools::web_fetch::WebFetchTool::from_config(&config.tools.web.fetch)
   778→        {
   779→            #[cfg(feature = "firecrawl")]
   780→            let t = t.with_firecrawl(&config.tools.web.firecrawl);
   781→            tool_registry.register(Box::new(t));
   782→        }
   783→        #[cfg(feature = "firecrawl")]
   784→        if let Some(t) =
   785→            moltis_tools::firecrawl::FirecrawlScrapeTool::from_config(&config.tools.web.firecrawl)
   786→        {
   787→            tool_registry.register(Box::new(t));
   788→        }
   789→        if let Some(t) = moltis_tools::browser::BrowserTool::from_config(&config.tools.browser) {
   790→            let t = if sandbox_router.backend_name() != "none" {
   791→                t.with_sandbox_router(Arc::clone(&sandbox_router))
   792→            } else {
   793→                t
   794→            };
   795→            tool_registry.register(Box::new(t));
   796→        }
   797→
   798→        #[cfg(feature = "caldav")]
   799→        {
   800→            if let Some(t) = moltis_caldav::tool::CalDavTool::from_config(&config.caldav) {
   801→                tool_registry.register(Box::new(t));
   802→            }
   803→        }
   804→
   805→        if let Some(ref mm) = memory_manager {
   806→            tool_registry.register(Box::new(moltis_memory::tools::MemorySearchTool::new(
   807→                Arc::clone(mm),
   808→            )));
   809→            tool_registry.register(Box::new(moltis_memory::tools::MemoryGetTool::new(
   810→                Arc::clone(mm),
   811→            )));
   812→            tool_registry.register(Box::new(moltis_memory::tools::MemorySaveTool::new(
   813→                Arc::clone(mm),
   814→            )));
   815→            tool_registry.register(Box::new(moltis_memory::tools::MemoryDeleteTool::new(
   816→                Arc::clone(mm),
   817→            )));
   818→            tool_registry.register(Box::new(moltis_chat::MemoryForgetTool::new(
   819→                Arc::clone(mm),
   820→                Arc::clone(&registry),
   821→                Arc::clone(&session_metadata),
   822→            )));
   823→        }
   824→
   825→        // ── Code index tools ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   826→        #[cfg(feature = "qmd")]
   827→        {
   828→            moltis_code_index::tools::register_tools(&mut tool_registry, code_index_for_tools);
   829→        }
   830→
   831→        #[cfg(all(feature = "code-index-builtin", not(feature = "qmd")))]
   832→        {
   833→            moltis_code_index::tools::register_tools(
   834→                &mut tool_registry,
   835→                code_index_for_tools_builtin,
   836→            );
   837→        }
   838→
   839→        {
   840→            let node_info_provider: Arc<dyn moltis_tools::nodes::NodeInfoProvider> =
   841→                Arc::new(crate::node_exec::GatewayNodeInfoProvider::new(
   842→                    Arc::clone(&state),
   843→                    config.tools.exec.ssh_target.clone(),
   844→                ));
   845→            tool_registry.register(Box::new(moltis_tools::nodes::NodesListTool::new(
   846→                Arc::clone(&node_info_provider),
   847→            )));
   848→            tool_registry.register(Box::new(moltis_tools::nodes::NodesDescribeTool::new(
   849→                Arc::clone(&node_info_provider),
   850→            )));
   851→            tool_registry.register(Box::new(moltis_tools::nodes::NodesSelectTool::new(
   852→                Arc::clone(&node_info_provider),
   853→            )));
   854→        }
   855→
   856→        tool_registry.register(Box::new(
   857→            moltis_tools::session_state::SessionStateTool::new(Arc::clone(&session_state_store)),
   858→        ));
   859→
   860→        let state_for_session_create = Arc::clone(&state);
   861→        let metadata_for_session_create = Arc::clone(&session_metadata);
   862→        let create_session: moltis_tools::sessions_manage::CreateSessionFn = Arc::new(
   863→            move |req: moltis_tools::sessions_manage::CreateSessionRequest| {
   864→                let state = Arc::clone(&state_for_session_create);
   865→                let metadata = Arc::clone(&metadata_for_session_create);
   866→                Box::pin(async move {
   867→                    let key = req.key;
   868→
   869→                    let mut resolve_params = serde_json::json!({ "key": key.clone() });
   870→                    if let Some(inherit) = req.inherit_agent_from {
   871→                        resolve_params["inherit_agent_from"] = serde_json::json!(inherit);
   872→                    }
   873→                    state
   874→                        .services
   875→                        .session
   876→                        .resolve(resolve_params)
   877→                        .await
   878→                        .map_err(|e| moltis_tools::Error::message(e.to_string()))?;
   879→
   880→                    let mut patch = serde_json::Map::new();
   881→                    patch.insert("key".to_string(), serde_json::json!(key.clone()));
   882→                    if let Some(label) = req.label {
   883→                        patch.insert("label".to_string(), serde_json::json!(label));
   884→                    }
   885→                    if let Some(model) = req.model {
   886→                        patch.insert("model".to_string(), serde_json::json!(model));
   887→                    }
   888→                    if let Some(project_id) = req.project_id {
   889→                        patch.insert("projectId".to_string(), serde_json::json!(project_id));
   890→                    }
   891→                    if patch.len() > 1 {
   892→                        state
   893→                            .services
   894→                            .session
   895→                            .patch(serde_json::Value::Object(patch))
   896→                            .await
   897→                            .map_err(|e| moltis_tools::Error::message(e.to_string()))?;
   898→                    }
   899→
   900→                    let entry = metadata.get(&key).await.ok_or_else(|| {
   901→                        moltis_tools::Error::message(format!(
   902→                            "session '{key}' not found after create"
   903→                        ))
   904→                    })?;
   905→                    Ok(serde_json::json!({
   906→                        "entry": {
   907→                            "id": entry.id,
   908→                            "key": entry.key,
   909→                            "label": entry.label,
   910→                            "model": entry.model,
   911→                            "createdAt": entry.created_at,
   912→                            "updatedAt": entry.updated_at,
   913→                            "messageCount": entry.message_count,
   914→                            "projectId": entry.project_id,
   915→                            "agent_id": entry.agent_id,
   916→                            "agentId": entry.agent_id,
   917→                            "version": entry.version,
   918→                        }
   919→                    }))
   920→                })
   921→            },
   922→        );
   923→
   924→        let state_for_session_delete = Arc::clone(&state);
   925→        let delete_session: moltis_tools::sessions_manage::DeleteSessionFn = Arc::new(
   926→            move |req: moltis_tools::sessions_manage::DeleteSessionRequest| {
   927→                let state = Arc::clone(&state_for_session_delete);
   928→                Box::pin(async move {
   929→                    state
   930→                        .services
   931→                        .session
   932→                        .delete(serde_json::json!({
   933→                            "key": req.key,
   934→                            "force": req.force,
   935→                        }))
   936→                        .await
   937→                        .map_err(|e| moltis_tools::Error::message(e.to_string()))
   938→                })
   939→            },
   940→        );
   941→
   942→        tool_registry.register(Box::new(
   943→            moltis_tools::sessions_manage::SessionsCreateTool::new(
   944→                Arc::clone(&session_metadata),
   945→                create_session,
   946→            ),
   947→        ));
   948→        tool_registry.register(Box::new(
   949→            moltis_tools::sessions_manage::SessionsDeleteTool::new(
   950→                Arc::clone(&session_metadata),
   951→                delete_session,
   952→            ),
   953→        ));
   954→        tool_registry.register(Box::new(
   955→            moltis_tools::sessions_communicate::SessionsListTool::new(Arc::clone(
   956→                &session_metadata,
   957→            )),
   958→        ));
   959→        tool_registry.register(Box::new(
   960→            moltis_tools::sessions_communicate::SessionsHistoryTool::new(
   961→                Arc::clone(&session_store),
   962→                Arc::clone(&session_metadata),
   963→            ),
   964→        ));
   965→        tool_registry.register(Box::new(
   966→            moltis_tools::sessions_communicate::SessionsSearchTool::new(
   967→                Arc::clone(&session_store),
   968→                Arc::clone(&session_metadata),
   969→            ),
   970→        ));
   971→
   972→        let state_for_session_send = Arc::clone(&state);
   973→        let send_to_session: moltis_tools::sessions_communicate::SendToSessionFn = Arc::new(
   974→            move |req: moltis_tools::sessions_communicate::SendToSessionRequest| {
   975→                let state = Arc::clone(&state_for_session_send);

## Test Files for moltis-config Crate

Here's a thorough breakdown of test files and patterns used for validation, semantic checking, schema mapping, and config parsing:

### Test Patterns

#### 1. **Unknown Field Detection**

Signature pattern:
```rust
fn unknown_top_level_key_with_suggestion() {
    let result = validate_toml_str("sever = 42\n");
    let unknown = result
        .diagnostics
        .iter()
        .find(|d| d.category == "unknown-field" && d.path == "sever");
    assert!(unknown.is_some(), "expected unknown-field diagnostic");
    assert!(d.message.contains("server"), "expected suggestion in message");
}
```

Tests across all validation modules validate:
- Top-level unknown fields: `common.rs:unknown_top_level_key_with_suggestion()`
- Nested unknown fields: `common.rs:unknown_nested_key_with_suggestion()`
- Unknown fields in dynamic maps (providers): `providers.rs:unknown_field_inside_provider_entry()`
- Nested in array entries: `structural.rs:hooks_array_entries_validated()`
- Nested in MCP servers: `structural.rs:mcp_server_entries_validated()`

#### 2. **Schema Drift Guard**

Signature pattern:
```rust
fn schema_drift_guard() {
    let config = MoltisConfig::default();
    let toml_value = toml::Value::try_from(&config).expect("serialize default config");
    let schema = build_schema_map();
    let mut missing = Vec::new();
    collect_missing_keys(&toml_value, &schema, "", &mut missing);
    assert!(missing.is_empty(), "schema map is missing keys...");
}
```

Helper function `collect_missing_keys()` recursively validates that `build_schema_map()` includes all fields present in `MoltisConfig::default()`.

#### 3. **Semantic/Type Validation**

Pattern for checking enum variants and valid values:
```rust
fn reasoning_effort_valid_values_no_error() {
    for effort in &["low", "medium", "high"] {
        let toml = format!(r#"[agents.presets.thinker]
reasoning_effort = "{effort}""#);
        let result = validate_toml_str(&toml);
        let errors = result.diagnostics.iter()
            .filter(|d| d.path.contains("reasoning_effort") && d.severity == Severity::Error)
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
        assert!(errors.is_empty(), "effort={effort} should be valid");
    }
}

fn reasoning_effort_invalid_value_reports_type_error() {
    let result = validate_toml_str(toml);
    let error = result.diagnostics.iter()
        .find(|d| d.category == "type-error" && d.severity == Severity::Error);
    assert!(error.is_some(), "invalid reasoning_effort should produce type error");
}
```

#### 4. **Suggestion Matching (Levenshtein)**

Signature patterns:
```rust
fn suggest_finds_close_match() {
    let candidates = &["server", "providers", "auth", "tls"];
    assert_eq!(suggest("sever", candidates, 3), Some("server"));
    assert_eq!(suggest("provders", candidates, 3), Some("providers"));
}

fn suggest_returns_none_for_distant() {
    assert_eq!(suggest("xxxxxxxxx", candidates, 3), None);
}
```

#### 5. **Provider-Specific Validation**

Pattern for provider name warnings and aliases:
```rust
fn misspelled_provider_name_warned_with_suggestion() {
    let toml = r#"[providers.anthrpic]
enabled = true"#;
    let result = validate_toml_str(toml);
    let warning = result.diagnostics.iter()
        .find(|d| d.category == "unknown-provider" && d.path == "providers.anthrpic");
    assert!(warning.is_some());
    assert_eq!(warning.severity, Severity::Warning);
    assert!(d.message.contains("anthropic"));
}

fn custom_provider_name_warned_without_close_match() {
    let result = validate_toml_str(r#"[providers.my_custom_llm]
enabled = true"#);
    let warning = result.diagnostics.iter()
        .find(|d| d.category == "unknown-provider");
    assert!(warning.is_some());
    assert!(d.message.contains("custom providers are valid"));
}
```

#### 6. **Security Warnings**

Pattern for config-level security checks:
```rust
fn auth_disabled_non_localhost_warned() {
    let toml = r#"[server]
bind = "0.0.0.0"
[auth]
disabled = true"#;
    let result = validate_toml_str(toml);
    let warning = result.diagnostics.iter()
        .find(|d| d.category == "security" && d.path == "auth");
    assert!(warning.is_some());
}

fn auth_disabled_localhost_not_warned() {
    let toml = r#"[server]
bind = "127.0.0.1"
[auth]
disabled = true"#;
    let result = validate_toml_str(toml);
    let warning = result.diagnostics.iter()
        .find(|d| d.category == "security" && d.path == "auth");
    assert!(warning.is_none());
}

fn tls_cert_without_key_is_error() {
    let result = validate_toml_str(r#"[tls]
cert_path = "/path/to/cert.pem""#);
    let error = result.diagnostics.iter().find(|d| {
        d.severity == Severity::Error && d.path == "tls" && d.message.contains("key_path")
    });
    assert!(error.is_some());
}
```

#### 7. **Deprecated Field Handling**

Pattern for warning on deprecated aliases:
```rust
fn memory_embedding_legacy_aliases_map_to_current_fields() {
    let config: MoltisConfig = toml::from_str(r#"
[memory]
embedding_provider = "custom"
embedding_base_url = "http://moltis-embeddings:7997/v1"
embedding_model = "intfloat/multilingual-e5-small"
embedding_api_key = "secret-key""#).unwrap();

    assert_eq!(config.memory.provider, Some(MemoryProvider::Custom));
    assert_eq!(config.memory.base_url.as_deref(), Some("http://moltis-embeddings:7997/v1"));
}
```

#### 8. **Config Parsing & Defaults**

Pattern from schema/tests.rs:
```rust
fn empty_config_is_valid() {
    let result = validate_toml_str("");
    assert!(!result.has_errors(), "empty config should be valid");
}

fn full_valid_config_no_diagnostics() {
    let toml = r#"[server]
bind = "127.0.0.1"
port = 8080
..."#;
    let result = validate_toml_str(toml);
    let errors = result.diagnostics.iter()
        .filter(|d| d.severity == Severity::Error).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    assert!(errors.is_empty(), "expected no errors for valid config");
}

fn memory_config_default_backend_is_builtin() {
    let cfg = MemoryEmbeddingConfig::default();
    assert_eq!(cfg.backend, MemoryBackend::Builtin);
}

fn providers_config_local_alias_maps_local_llm_to_local() {
    let mut config = ProvidersConfig::default();
    config.providers.insert("local-llm".into(), ProviderEntry { enabled: false, .. });
    assert!(!config.is_enabled("local"));
    assert!(!config.is_enabled("local-llm"));
    assert!(config.get("local").is_some());
}
```

#### 9. **Environment Override Tests**

Pattern from loader/tests.rs:
```rust
fn apply_env_overrides_auth_disabled() {
    let vars = vec![("MOLTIS_AUTH__DISABLED".into(), "true".into())];
    let config = MoltisConfig::default();
    assert!(!config.auth.disabled);
    let config = apply_env_overrides_with(config, vars.into_iter());
    assert!(config.auth.disabled);
}

fn apply_env_overrides_providers_offered_array() {
    let vars = vec![("MOLTIS_PROVIDERS__OFFERED".into(), "[\"openai\",\"github-copilot\"]".into())];
    let config = apply_env_overrides_with(MoltisConfig::default(), vars.into_iter());
    assert_eq!(config.providers.offered, vec!["openai", "github-copilot"]);
}
```

#### 10. **KnownKeys Schema Map Pattern**

The schema map uses a declarative enum structure:
```rust
pub(super) enum KnownKeys {
    Struct(HashMap<&'static str, KnownKeys>),  // Fixed fields
    Map(Box<KnownKeys>),                        // Dynamic keys (e.g., providers.*)
    MapWithFields { value: Box<KnownKeys>, fields: HashMap<&'static str, KnownKeys> },  // Dynamic + static
    Array(Box<KnownKeys>),                      // Array items
    Leaf,                                       // Scalar — stop recursion
}
```

### Core Validation Flow

The validation pipeline in `validate_toml_str()` follows this order:
1. **Syntax** — Parse raw TOML
2. **Unknown fields** — Walk against `build_schema_map()` via `check_unknown_fields()`
3. **Deprecation warnings** — Check for legacy aliases via `check_deprecated_fields()`
4. **Provider hints** — Validate provider names via `check_provider_names()` with Levenshtein matching
5. **Type check** — Full deserialization of `MoltisConfig`
6. **Semantic warnings** — Config-level checks like `auth.disabled + non-localhost` via `check_semantic_warnings()`

All diagnostics have:
- `severity: Severity` (Error, Warning, Info)
- `category: &'static str` (unknown-field, type-error, deprecated-field, unknown-provider, security, file-ref)
- `path: String` (dotted notation, e.g., "server.bnd", "providers.anthropic.api_ky", "channels.offered[1]")
- `message: String` (includes suggestions for unknown fields)
agentId: ae1a63282074f2f03 (use SendMessage with to: 'ae1a63282074f2f03' to continue this agent)
<usage>total_tokens: 63344
tool_uses: 21
duration_ms: 58220</usage>
```

   143→#[test]
   144→fn schema_drift_guard() {
   145→    let config = MoltisConfig::default();
   146→    let toml_value = toml::Value::try_from(&config).expect("serialize default config");
   147→    let schema = build_schema_map();
   148→    let mut missing = Vec::new();
   149→    collect_missing_keys(&toml_value, &schema, "", &mut missing);
   150→    assert!(
   151→        missing.is_empty(),
   152→        "schema map is missing keys present in MoltisConfig::default(): {missing:?}\n\
   153→         Update build_schema_map() in validate.rs to include these fields."
   154→    );
   155→}
   156→
   157→/// Helper for schema drift guard: recursively collect keys in `value` that
   158→/// are not present in `schema`.
   159→fn collect_missing_keys(
   160→    value: &toml::Value,
   161→    schema: &KnownKeys,
   162→    prefix: &str,
   163→    missing: &mut Vec<String>,
   164→) {
   165→    match (value, schema) {
   166→        (toml::Value::Table(table), KnownKeys::Struct(fields)) => {
   167→            for (key, child_value) in table {
   168→                let path = if prefix.is_empty() {
   169→                    key.clone()
   170→                } else {
   171→                    format!("{prefix}.{key}")
   172→                };
   173→                if let Some(child_schema) = fields.get(key.as_str()) {
   174→                    collect_missing_keys(child_value, child_schema, &path, missing);
   175→                } else {
   176→                    missing.push(path);
   177→                }
   178→            }
   179→        },
   180→        (toml::Value::Table(table), KnownKeys::Map(value_schema)) => {
   181→            for (key, child_value) in table {
   182→                let path = if prefix.is_empty() {
   183→                    key.clone()
   184→                } else {
   185→                    format!("{prefix}.{key}")
   186→                };
   187→                collect_missing_keys(child_value, value_schema, &path, missing);
   188→            }
   189→        },
   190→        (
   191→            toml::Value::Table(table),
   192→            KnownKeys::MapWithFields {
   193→                value: value_schema,
   194→                fields,
   195→            },
   196→        ) => {
   197→            for (key, child_value) in table {
   198→                let path = if prefix.is_empty() {
   199→                    key.clone()
   200→                } else {
   201→                    format!("{prefix}.{key}")
   202→                };
   203→                if let Some(child_schema) = fields.get(key.as_str()) {
   204→                    collect_missing_keys(child_value, child_schema, &path, missing);
   205→                } else {
   206→                    collect_missing_keys(child_value, value_schema, &path, missing);
   207→                }
   208→            }
   209→        },
   210→        (toml::Value::Array(arr), KnownKeys::Array(item_schema)) => {
   211→            for (i, item) in arr.iter().enumerate() {
   212→                let path = format!("{prefix}[{i}]");
   213→                collect_missing_keys(item, item_schema, &path, missing);
   214→            }
   215→        },
   216→        _ => {},
   217→    }
   218→}
   219→
   220→#[test]
   221→fn suggest_finds_close_match() {
   222→    let candidates = &["server", "providers", "auth", "tls"];
   223→    assert_eq!(suggest("sever", candidates, 3), Some("server"));
   224→    assert_eq!(suggest("servar", candidates, 3), Some("server"));
   225→    assert_eq!(suggest("provders", candidates, 3), Some("providers"));
   226→}
   227→
   228→#[test]
   229→fn suggest_returns_none_for_distant() {
   230→    let candidates = &["server", "providers", "auth", "tls"];
   231→    assert_eq!(suggest("xxxxxxxxx", candidates, 3), None);
   232→}
   233→

   242→    let unknown = result

   246→    assert!(
   247→        unknown.is_none(),
   248→        "terminal_enabled should be a known field, got: {unknown:?}"
   249→    );
   250→}

When the env var is set\n    /// to `\"1\"` or `\"true\"` (case-insensitive), the terminal is disabled\n    /// regardless of the config file value.\n    pub fn is_terminal_enabled(&self) -> bool {\n        if let Ok(val) = std::env::var(\"MOLTIS_TERMINAL_DISABLED\")\n            && (val.eq_ignore_ascii_case(\"true\") || val == \"1\")\n        {\n            return false;\n        }\n        self.terminal_enabled\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "    /// Returns whether the web UI terminal is enabled, accounting for the\n    /// `MOLTIS_TERMINAL_DISABLED` env-var override. When the env var is set\n    /// to `\"1\"` or `\"true\"` (case-insensitive), the terminal is disabled\n    /// regardless of the config file value.\n    pub fn is_terminal_enabled(&self) -> bool {\n        if let Ok(val) = std::env::var(\"MOLTIS_TERMINAL_DISABLED\")\n            && (val.eq_ignore_ascii_case(\"true\") || val == \"1\")\n        {\n            return false;\n        }\n        self.terminal_enabled\n    }\n\n    /// Returns the effective external URL, checking `MOLTIS_EXTERNAL_URL`\n    /// env var first, then falling back to the config field. Trailing\n    /// slashes are stripped so the result can be used directly as a\n    /// WebAuthn origin.\n    pub fn effective_external_url(&self) -> Option<String> {\n        let raw = std::env::var(\"MOLTIS_EXTERNAL_URL\")\n            .ok()\n            .filter(|v| !v.is_empty())\n            .or_else(|| self.external_url.clone())?;\n        Some(raw.trim_end_matches('/').to_string())\n    }\n}"
}

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_018LhM15bvh62HN5g5rcZznC
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/stone-wood/crates/gateway/src/server/prepare_core/post_state.rs",
  "old_string": "    let explicit_rp_id = std::env::var(\"MOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_RP_ID\")\n        .or_else(|_| std::env::var(\"APP_DOMAIN\"))\n        .or_else(|_| std::env::var(\"RENDER_EXTERNAL_HOSTNAME\"))\n        .or_else(|_| std::env::var(\"FLY_APP_NAME\").map(|name| format!(\"{name}.fly.dev\")))\n        .or_else(|_| std::env::var(\"RAILWAY_PUBLIC_DOMAIN\"))\n        .ok();\n    let explicit_origin = std::env::var(\"MOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_ORIGIN\")\n        .or_else(|_| std::env::var(\"APP_URL\"))\n        .or_else(|_| std::env::var(\"RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL\"))\n        .ok();",
  "new_string": "    // Derive RP ID and origin from server.external_url / MOLTIS_EXTERNAL_URL\n    // when available, before falling back to fine-grained env vars.\n    let (external_rp_id, external_origin) =\n        if let Some(ref ext_url) = config.server.effective_external_url() {\n            match url::Url::parse(ext_url) {\n                Ok(parsed) => {\n                    let host = parsed.host_str().unwrap_or_default().to_string();\n                    (Some(host), Some(ext_url.clone()))\n                },\n                Err(e) => {\n                    warn!(\"invalid server.external_url '{ext_url}': {e}\");\n                    (None, None)\n                },\n            }\n        } else {\n            (None, None)\n        };\n\n    let explicit_rp_id = external_rp_id\n        .or_else(|| std::env::var(\"MOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_RP_ID\").ok())\n        .or_else(|| std::env::var(\"APP_DOMAIN\").ok())\n        .or_else(|| std::env::var(\"RENDER_EXTERNAL_HOSTNAME\").ok())\n        .or_else(|| std::env::var(\"FLY_APP_NAME\").ok().map(|name| format!(\"{name}.fly.dev\")))\n        .or_else(|| std::env::var(\"RAILWAY_PUBLIC_DOMAIN\").ok());\n    let explicit_origin = external_origin\n        .or_else(|| std::env::var(\"MOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_ORIGIN\").ok())\n        .or_else(|| std::env::var(\"APP_URL\").ok())\n        .or_else(|| std::env::var(\"RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL\").ok());"
}

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/stone-wood/docs/src/security.md",
  "old_string": "### Passkeys Behind Proxies (Host Changes)\n\nWebAuthn passkeys are bound to an RP ID (domain identity), not just the\nserver process. In practice:\n\n- If users move from one hostname to another, old passkeys for the old host\n  will not authenticate on the new host.\n- If a proxy rewrites `Host` and does not preserve browser host context,\n  passkey routes can fail with \"no passkey config for this hostname\".\n\nFor stable proxy deployments, set explicit WebAuthn identity to your public\ndomain:\n\n```bash\nMOLTIS_BEHIND_PROXY=true\nMOLTIS_NO_TLS=true\nMOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_RP_ID=chat.example.com\nMOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_ORIGIN=https://chat.example.com\n```\n\nMigration guidance when changing host/domain:\n\n1. Keep password login enabled during migration.\n2. Deploy with the new `MOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_RP_ID`/`MOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_ORIGIN`.\n3. Ask users to register a new passkey on the new host.\n4. Remove old passkeys after new-host login is confirmed.",
  "new_string": "### Passkeys Behind Proxies (Host Changes)\n\nWebAuthn passkeys are bound to an RP ID (domain identity), not just the\nserver process. In practice:\n\n- If users move from one hostname to another, old passkeys for the old host\n  will not authenticate on the new host.\n- If a proxy rewrites `Host` and does not preserve browser host context,\n  passkey routes can fail with \"no passkey config for this hostname\".\n\nFor stable proxy deployments, set your public URL in `moltis.toml`:\n\n```toml\n[server]\nexternal_url = \"https://chat.example.com\"\n```\n\nMoltis derives the WebAuthn RP ID (domain) and origin from this URL\nautomatically. The `MOLTIS_EXTERNAL_URL` environment variable takes\nprecedence over the config field, which is useful for container\ndeployments:\n\n```bash\nMOLTIS_BEHIND_PROXY=true\nMOLTIS_NO_TLS=true\nMOLTIS_EXTERNAL_URL=https://chat.example.com\n```\n\nFor fine-grained control (e.g. when the RP ID differs from the URL host),\nthe existing env vars still work and take precedence after\n`external_url`:\n\n```bash\nMOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_RP_ID=chat.example.com\nMOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_ORIGIN=https://chat.example.com\n```\n\nMigration guidance when changing host/domain:\n\n1. Keep password login enabled during migration.\n2. Deploy with the new `server.external_url` (or env var equivalent).\n3. Ask users to register a new passkey on the new host.\n4. Remove old passkeys after new-host login is confirmed."
}
```

   518→fn tool_mode_serde_round_trip() {

   520→        (ToolMode::Auto, r#""auto""#),
   521→        (ToolMode::Native, r#""native""#),
   522→        (ToolMode::Text, r#""text""#),
   523→        (ToolMode::Off, r#""off""#),
   524→    ] {

   526→        assert_eq!(json, expected_str, "serialize {variant:?}");
   527→        let parsed: ToolMode = serde_json::from_str(&json).unwrap();
   528→        assert_eq!(parsed, variant, "deserialize {expected_str}");
   529→    }
   530→}

   532→#[test]
   533→fn tool_mode_toml_round_trip() {
   534→    #[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
   535→    struct Wrapper {
   536→        mode: ToolMode,
   537→    }
   538→
   539→    for variant in [
   540→        ToolMode::Auto,
   541→        ToolMode::Native,
   542→        ToolMode::Text,
   543→        ToolMode::Off,
   544→    ] {
   545→        let w = Wrapper { mode: variant };
   546→        let toml_str = toml::to_string(&w).unwrap();
   547→        let parsed: Wrapper = toml::from_str(&toml_str).unwrap();
   548→        assert_eq!(parsed.mode, variant, "toml round-trip {variant:?}");
   549→    }
   550→}
