```
   921→
   922→/// GH-684: section order must be preserved after a save roundtrip.
   923→#[test]
   924→fn gh684_template_section_order_preserved_after_save() {
   925→    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().expect("tempdir");
   926→    let path = dir.path().join("moltis.toml");
   927→    let template = crate::template::default_config_template(18789);
   928→    std::fs::write(&path, &template).expect("write template");
   929→
   930→    let template_sections: Vec<String> = template
   931→        .lines()
   932→        .filter(|l| l.starts_with('['))
   933→        .map(|l| l.to_string())
   934→        .collect();
   935→
   936→    // Load, modify, save — simulating a web UI setting change
   937→    let raw = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).expect("read");
   938→    let mut config: MoltisConfig = parse_config(&raw, &path).expect("parse");
   939→    config.auth.disabled = true;
   940→    config.server.http_request_logs = true;
   941→
   942→    save_config_to_path(&path, &config).expect("save config");
   943→
   944→    let saved = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).expect("read saved");
   945→    let saved_sections: Vec<String> = saved
   946→        .lines()
   947→        .filter(|l| l.starts_with('['))
   948→        .map(|l| l.to_string())
   949→        .collect();
   950→
   951→    // Every template section must still exist in the saved file
   952→    for ts in &template_sections {
   953→        assert!(
   954→            saved_sections.contains(ts),
   955→            "template section {ts} missing from saved file"
   956→        );
   957→    }
   958→
   959→    // Template sections must maintain their relative order
   960→    let template_positions: Vec<usize> = template_sections
   961→        .iter()
   962→        .map(|ts| {
   963→            saved_sections
   964→                .iter()
   965→                .position(|ss| ss == ts)
   966→                .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("section {ts} not found in saved"))
   967→        })
   968→        .collect();
   969→    for window in template_positions.windows(2) {
   970→        assert!(
   971→            window[0] < window[1],
   972→            "template sections swapped: saved position {} >= {} \
   973→                 (sections around index {})",
   974→            window[0],
   975→            window[1],
   976→            window[0],
   977→        );
   978→    }
   979→}
   980→
   981→/// GH-684: new sub-tables must render after their parent, not interleaved
   982→/// with unrelated sections.
   983→#[test]
   984→fn gh684_new_subtables_render_after_parent() {
   985→    let original = r#"[server]
   986→port = 8080
   987→
   988→[channels]
   989→offered = ["telegram"]
   990→
   991→[memory]
   992→enabled = true
   993→"#;
   994→
   995→    let updated = r#"[server]
   996→port = 8080
   997→
   998→[channels]
   999→offered = ["telegram"]
  1000→
  1001→[channels.telegram]
  1002→token = "abc"
  1003→
  1004→[channels.whatsapp]
  1005→token = "xyz"
  1006→
  1007→[memory]
  1008→enabled = true
  1009→"#;
  1010→
  1011→    let mut current_doc = original.parse::<toml_edit::DocumentMut>().unwrap();
  1012→    let updated_doc = updated.parse::<toml_edit::DocumentMut>().unwrap();
  1013→
  1014→    merge_toml_tables(current_doc.as_table_mut(), updated_doc.as_table());
  1015→    let result = current_doc.to_string();
  1016→
  1017→    let sections: Vec<&str> = result.lines().filter(|l| l.starts_with('[')).collect();
  1018→
  1019→    // Expected order: server, channels, channels.telegram, channels.whatsapp, memory
  1020→    let channels_idx = sections.iter().position(|s| *s == "[channels]").unwrap();
  1021→    let telegram_idx = sections
  1022→        .iter()
  1023→        .position(|s| *s == "[channels.telegram]")
  1024→        .unwrap();
  1025→    let whatsapp_idx = sections
  1026→        .iter()
  1027→        .position(|s| *s == "[channels.whatsapp]")
  1028→        .unwrap();
  1029→    let memory_idx = sections.iter().position(|s| *s == "[memory]").unwrap();
  1030→
  1031→    assert!(
  1032→        channels_idx < telegram_idx,
  1033→        "[channels.telegram] must come after [channels]"
  1034→    );
  1035→    assert!(
  1036→        telegram_idx < whatsapp_idx,
  1037→        "[channels.whatsapp] must come after [channels.telegram]"
  1038→    );
  1039→    assert!(
  1040→        whatsapp_idx < memory_idx,
  1041→        "[memory] must come after channel sub-tables"
  1042→    );
  1043→}

  1045→#[test]
```

```json
  "new_string": "fn apply_env_overrides_ignores_excluded() {\n    // MOLTIS_CONFIG_DIR should not be treated as a config field override.\n    let vars = vec![(\"MOLTIS_CONFIG_DIR\".into(), \"/tmp/test\".into())];\n    let config = apply_env_overrides_with(MoltisConfig::default(), vars.into_iter());\n    assert!(!config.auth.disabled);\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn apply_env_overrides_ignores_external_url() {\n    // MOLTIS_EXTERNAL_URL is handled by effective_external_url(), not by\n    // the generic env override mechanism.\n    let vars = vec![(\n        \"MOLTIS_EXTERNAL_URL\".into(),\n        \"https://test.example.com\".into(),\n    )];\n    let config = apply_env_overrides_with(MoltisConfig::default(), vars.into_iter());\n    assert!(\n        config.server.external_url.is_none(),\n        \"MOLTIS_EXTERNAL_URL should be excluded from generic env overrides\"\n    );\n}"
```

> AGENT

All 11 files modified as planned, with 249 insertions and 13 deletions (the deletions are from replacing the old WebAuthn env var chain). Let me do a final summary of what was implemented:

## Summary

Implemented `server.external_url` config field across 7 areas:

1. **Config schema** (`crates/config/src/schema/system.rs`) — Added `external_url: Option<String>` field to `ServerConfig` with `effective_external_url()` helper that checks `MOLTIS_EXTERNAL_URL` env var first, then config value, stripping trailing slashes.

2. **Schema map** (`crates/config/src/validate/schema_map.rs`) — Registered `("external_url", Leaf)` in the `server` struct.

3. **Semantic validation** (`crates/config/src/validate/semantic.rs`) — Error for non-http/https schemes, warning for trailing slashes.

4. **Config template** (`crates/config/src/template.rs`) — Added commented-out `external_url` with documentation.

5. **Env var exclusion** (`crates/config/src/loader/config_io.rs`) — Added `MOLTIS_EXTERNAL_URL` to the `EXCLUDED` list to prevent double-processing.

6. **WebAuthn wiring** (`crates/gateway/src/server/prepare_core/post_state.rs`) — `effective_external_url()` is checked first, URL is parsed to extract host (RP ID) and full URL (origin). Falls through to existing env var chain if unset.

7. **Documentation** (`docs/src/security.md`) — Updated "Passkeys Behind Proxies" section to recommend `server.external_url` as the primary approach.

**Tests added** (13 new tests across 4 files):
- Config parsing: defaults to `None`, parses from TOML
- `effective_external_url()`: returns config value, strips trailing slash, returns `None` when unset
- Validation: bad scheme → error, trailing slash → warning, valid http/https → clean
- Schema map: `external_url` recognized as known field
- Env override exclusion: `MOLTIS_EXTERNAL_URL` not processed by generic mechanism

```json
{
  "command": "git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nfeat(config): add server.external_url for reverse proxy WebAuthn\n\nUsers running moltis behind a reverse proxy with SSL termination\n(e.g. https://moltis.example.com) had no discoverable way to tell\nmoltis its public URL. The only workaround was setting env vars\n(MOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_RP_ID + MOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_ORIGIN), which wasn't\ndocumented in the config file.\n\nAdd a first-class `server.external_url` config field that:\n- Derives WebAuthn RP ID (host) and origin from the URL\n- Supports MOLTIS_EXTERNAL_URL env var override (highest priority)\n- Falls through to existing env var chain if unset\n- Validates scheme (http/https only) and warns on trailing slashes\n- Is excluded from the generic MOLTIS_ env override mechanism\n\nPriority order for WebAuthn RP registration:\n1. server.external_url / MOLTIS_EXTERNAL_URL (new)\n2. MOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_RP_ID + MOLTIS_WEBAUTHN_ORIGIN (existing)\n3. Cloud platform env vars: APP_DOMAIN, RENDER_*, FLY_*, RAILWAY_*\n4. Localhost/hostname auto-detection\n\nCloses #782\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Commit with conventional commit message"
}
```

Start gateway with `[server] external_url = \"https://test.example.com\"` — verify startup log shows `WebAuthn RP registered` with RP ID `test.example.com` and origin `https://test.example.com`\n2. Set `MOLTIS_EXTERNAL_URL=https://env.example.com` env var alongside the config field — verify the env var wins (RP ID = `env.example.com`)\n3. Remove both config field and env var — verify localhost/hostname auto-detection still works as before\n4. Set `external_url = \"ftp://bad.example.com\"` — verify `moltis config validate` reports an error\n5. Set `external_url = \"https://example.com/\"` — verify validation warns about trailing slash\nEOF\n)\"",

```
    86→impl ServerConfig {
    87→    /// Returns whether the web UI terminal is enabled, accounting for the
    88→    /// `MOLTIS_TERMINAL_DISABLED` env-var override. When the env var is set
    89→    /// to `"1"` or `"true"` (case-insensitive), the terminal is disabled
    90→    /// regardless of the config file value.
    91→    pub fn is_terminal_enabled(&self) -> bool {
    92→        if let Ok(val) = std::env::var("MOLTIS_TERMINAL_DISABLED")
    93→            && (val.eq_ignore_ascii_case("true") || val == "1")
    94→        {
    95→            return false;
    96→        }
    97→        self.terminal_enabled
    98→    }
    99→
   100→    /// Returns the effective external URL, checking `MOLTIS_EXTERNAL_URL`
   101→    /// env var first, then falling back to the config field. Trailing
   102→    /// slashes are stripped so the result can be used directly as a
   103→    /// WebAuthn origin.
   104→    pub fn effective_external_url(&self) -> Option<String> {
   105→        let raw = std::env::var("MOLTIS_EXTERNAL_URL")
   106→            .ok()
   107→            .filter(|v| !v.is_empty())
   108→            .or_else(|| self.external_url.clone())?;
   109→        Some(raw.trim_end_matches('/').to_string())

   228→    /// Rate limit window in seconds. Defaults to 60 (1 minute).

   230→    /// Number of days to retain cron session data before auto-cleanup.

   233→    /// Whether to auto-prune sandbox containers after cron job completion.

   237→    pub auto_prune_cron_containers: bool,
   238→}
```

```json
{
```
```json
  "replace_all": false,
```

```json
  "old_string": "    /// Returns the effective external URL, checking `MOLTIS_EXTERNAL_URL`\n    /// env var first, then falling back to the config field. Trailing\n    /// slashes are stripped so the result can be used directly as a\n    /// WebAuthn origin.\n    pub fn effective_external_url(&self) -> Option<String> {\n        let raw = std::env::var(\"MOLTIS_EXTERNAL_URL\")\n            .ok()\n            .filter(|v| !v.is_empty())\n            .or_else(|| self.external_url.clone())?;\n        Some(raw.trim_end_matches('/').to_string())\n    }",
  "new_string": "    /// Returns the effective external URL, checking `MOLTIS_EXTERNAL_URL`\n    /// env var first, then falling back to the config field. Trailing\n    /// slashes are stripped so the result can be used directly as a\n    /// WebAuthn origin.\n    pub fn effective_external_url(&self) -> Option<String> {\n        let env_val = std::env::var(\"MOLTIS_EXTERNAL_URL\")\n            .ok()\n            .filter(|v| !v.is_empty());\n        Self::resolve_external_url(env_val.as_deref(), self.external_url.as_deref())\n    }\n\n    /// Pure helper for [`effective_external_url`] — exposed for testing\n    /// without mutating process env vars.\n    pub fn resolve_external_url(\n        env_val: Option<&str>,\n        config_val: Option<&str>,\n    ) -> Option<String> {\n        let raw = env_val.or(config_val)?;\n        Some(raw.trim_end_matches('/').to_string())\n    }"
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/stone-wood/crates/config/src/schema/tests.rs",
  "old_string": "fn effective_external_url_returns_none_when_unset() {\n    let cfg: MoltisConfig = toml::from_str(\"\").unwrap();\n    assert!(cfg.server.effective_external_url().is_none());\n}",
  "new_string": "fn effective_external_url_returns_none_when_unset() {\n    let cfg: MoltisConfig = toml::from_str(\"\").unwrap();\n    assert!(cfg.server.effective_external_url().is_none());\n}\n\n// ── resolve_external_url (pure helper, testable without env mutation) ──\n\n#[test]\nfn resolve_external_url_env_takes_precedence_over_config() {\n    let result = ServerConfig::resolve_external_url(\n        Some(\"https://from-env.example.com\"),\n        Some(\"https://from-config.example.com\"),\n    );\n    assert_eq!(result.as_deref(), Some(\"https://from-env.example.com\"));\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn resolve_external_url_empty_env_falls_back_to_config() {\n    // effective_external_url filters empty env values before calling\n    // resolve_external_url, so an empty env var becomes None here.\n    let result = ServerConfig::resolve_external_url(\n        None,\n        Some(\"https://from-config.example.com\"),\n    );\n    assert_eq!(result.as_deref(), Some(\"https://from-config.example.com\"));\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn resolve_external_url_strips_trailing_slash_from_env() {\n    let result = ServerConfig::resolve_external_url(\n        Some(\"https://env.example.com/\"),\n        None,\n    );\n    assert_eq!(result.as_deref(), Some(\"https://env.example.com\"));\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn resolve_external_url_strips_trailing_slash_from_config() {\n    let result = ServerConfig::resolve_external_url(\n        None,\n        Some(\"https://config.example.com/\"),\n    );\n    assert_eq!(result.as_deref(), Some(\"https://config.example.com\"));\n}\n\n#[test]\nfn resolve_external_url_returns_none_when_both_unset() {\n    let result = ServerConfig::resolve_external_url(None, None);\n    assert!(result.is_none());\n}"
}
```

> AGENT

Both review comments addressed:

- **P1**: Added empty-host guard in `post_state.rs` — URLs like `file:///path` that parse successfully but have no hostname now warn and return `(None, None)`, falling through to localhost/hostname auto-detection instead of silently disabling passkey auth.

- **P2**: Extracted `resolve_external_url(env_val, config_val)` as a pure testable function (avoids unsafe `set_var`). Added 5 unit tests covering env precedence, empty-env fallback, trailing slash stripping from both sources, and both-unset returning `None`.
